# M3.2 Sleep UI Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (chosen by Andrew on 13 Oct 2026) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Both phones log sleep the way they log feeds: Start sleep and Wake up in two taps from Today, a past sleep, a nap or a night label, and every sleep on Today, History, Trends and Recently deleted.

**Architecture:**
- **Entries** (`apps/web/src/api/today-change.ts`, `swap.ts`): a sleep becomes the third kind of `RecentEntry`, so Today's list, the edit sheets' cached copies and History's details handle it through the same helpers (`recordOf`, `idOf`, `timeOf`, `dayCopy`, `todayCopy`). The Worker lists the last 24 hours' sleeps in Today's `recent`.
- **Actions** (`api/actions.ts`, `send.ts`, `today-cache.ts`, `sheets/*.ts`): M3.1's six endpoints become `SleepAction`s with the same treatment as a feed's: shown at once on Today and cached days, kept on the phone when new (M5.6), explained when refused, recovered while they still fit (M1.13), and undone from a toast.
- **Screens:** Today's sleep card and Sleep row (`features/today/`), the sleep sheet and the bar's fourth button, the past-sleep and edit-sleep sheets (`features/sleep/`), History's sleep column and Week stripe (`features/history/`), Trends' fifth chart (`features/trends/metrics.ts`, with `runningTotals` in `packages/domain`), and Recently deleted (`features/deleted/`).

**Tech Stack:** React 19.3.0, TypeScript 5.9.3, TanStack Router 1.170.41 and Query 5.104.1, `effect` 4.0.1 (Schema), `@bt/contracts` and `@bt/domain`, shadcn on Base UI 1.8.0 (`ToggleGroup`, `Button`, `Input`), Tailwind CSS 4.3.3, `lucide-react` 1.51.0 (`MoonStarIcon`), `@tanstack/charts` 1.1.0, Vitest 4.1.11 with fast-check 4.10.2, Playwright 1.64.0, oxlint 1.87.0 and oxfmt 0.72.0, Node 24.21.0. M3.2 adds no package.

**Spec:** `docs/implementation-plan.md`:
- the M3.2 row (this plan writes its decisions, Task 10); AC 2 (two taps), AC 12 and AC 21 through M3.1
- §3.1 (`trends/`, `calendar/` bullets), §5's "Start sleep while one is active" (P7)
- M3.1's plan, `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-13-m3.1-sleep-core.md` (fa's, approved 13 Oct 2026): `Sleep`, `SleepConflict`, the six endpoints and their rules, `sleepKindAt`, `Totals`' sleep fields, `Today.activeSleep` and `lastSleep`, `Deleted.sleeps`, the periods' `sleeps`

The hand-offs this plan takes, from #43 (M3.1's issue, posted on #44 by fa):
- **M1.11:** `{ kind: "sleep", sleep }` in `RecentEntry`, Today's recent query and its row. `packages/contracts/test/today.test.ts` used a sleep as "a kind this app doesn't know"; it now uses M3.3's dose (Task 1).
- **M1.12:** `sleep` in `SheetKind`, the endpoints in `Action` and `send`, and sentences for `SLEEP_ALREADY_ACTIVE` and `SLEEP_OVERLAP` (Task 2).
- **M5.6:** the sleep's new entries in `NEW_ENTRIES` and its sheets in `TODAY_SHEETS` (Task 2).
- **M3.7:** a sleep on the Day grid, with its own fill (Task 6, in a column of its own, as decided).
- **M3.8:** a sleep in the Week view's marks, `"sleep"` in `SHOWN_KINDS`, zeroed in `shownTotals`, and a legend entry (Tasks 6 and 7).
- **M3.10:** the Trends sleep chart; `hasEntries` already counts sleep (Task 8).
- **M3.5, the web half:** `restoreSleep` in `Action` and `send`, a row in `deletedRows`, and `restoreFailure`'s sentences (Tasks 2 and 9).

Andrew decided these on 13 Oct 2026, in the grill before this plan (taking every recommendation; recorded on #44):
- **Execution:** subagents. Built ahead of M1's week of use, on M3.1's contract; one plan and one PR, opened after M3.1's PR merges. Since M5.7 its merge deploys sleep to both phones.
- **Sleep on the quick-action bar.** A fourth button, Sleep, joins Bottle, Nursing and Diaper, and all four stack the icon over a smaller label so they fit a 360 px phone. Sleep opens the sleep sheet, as Nursing does: Start sleep, or Wake up while she's asleep, two taps each (AC 2). The button stays "Sleep". Measured in WebKit: "Nursing" needs about 96 px inline, and four buttons get 76 px each at 360 px.
- **Sleep in the totals and on Trends.** Today's totals gain a Sleep row, the time asleep with naps and night under it, and Trends a fifth chart in the same order and words: hours a day with Naps and Night stacked, its numbers beside it, the longest sleep among them. On the Day view, sleep climbs from midnight against her usual day, like the other four.
- **A column of its own on the calendar.** On the Day grid, sleeps get a narrow column between the hour labels and the feeds, as bottles and diapers keep theirs on the right, so feeds never change width. On the Week view, a thin sleep stripe runs down each column's left edge. Sleeps never overlap each other, so the column needs no lanes.
- **Feeds and sleeps stay independent.** Starting Nursing or Bottle never wakes her, and Wake up never touches a feed. A night feed sits inside the night's sleep, as D9 allows, and the night stays one sleep. A forgotten Wake up is fixed with Sleep, then Wake up at a changed time.

- **Lint:**
  - It bans comments of every kind (`gamesheet/no-comments`). Don't turn one into JSDoc or a `SAFETY:` comment; put the reason in a name or a test title.
  - No type assertions except `as const`, no non-null `!`, complexity at most 10 per function.
  - The anti-slop rules ban `unknown` and `object` parameters, `typeof` checks, "shape" in names, `vi.mock`, and an explicit type on a binding whose value already has one (use `satisfies`).
  - `jsx-a11y/label-has-associated-control`: a `label` names its control; `sleep-kind-toggle.tsx` names its group with `useId`.
  - E2E files have no DOM types, so `page.evaluate` takes a string when it reads `window` or `document`.
- **React:**
  - Components only display. Logic lives in `.ts` files with Node tests.
  - No `useEffect` and no `style` props. A grid item's position comes from M3.7's `cal-at`, `cal-tall` and `cal-lane*` utilities.
  - Keep each file under about 100 lines.
  - Before writing a task's components, invoke `frontend-ui-expert` and `react-code-standards` (Andrew's standing instruction). The design is fixed (M1.10's look and the decisions here), so use them to check the work, not to redesign it.
- **UI:** semantic tokens only, never raw colours, and no `dark:` in app code. A sleep is drawn in `muted-foreground` tints, apart from the feeds' `primary`. Buttons and chips outside the grid are at least 44 px. Icons from `lucide-react` with `aria-hidden="true"` beside a label; a sleep's icon is `MoonStarIcon`, apart from the dark theme's `MoonIcon`. Numbers use `tabular-nums`.
- **Words:** "Nap" and "Night" name a sleep; "Asleep now", "Since she woke", "Fell asleep", "Woke", "Start sleep", "Wake up", "Log a past sleep", "Past sleep", "Edit sleep", "Pick a time after she fell asleep.", "A sleep can last a day at most." The app calls the child "her" and "she", as Trends' "her usual day" does.

## Decisions this plan makes (veto in review)

- **P1: Today's sleep card** sits under the feed card, as a second `StatusCard`. Asleep: "● Asleep now · Nap", the running timer (`role="timer"`), "Fell asleep 8:47 AM". Awake: "Since she woke", the time since `lastSleep.endedAt` ("2 h 10 min", never below zero), and "Woke 7:20 AM · Nap 50 min". Before any sleep: "No sleeps yet" with "The first sleep you log will show here."
- **P2: Nap or night.** A new sleep's label follows the household's clock at the start shown (`sleepKindAt`: night from 7 PM to 6:59 AM) until a parent taps Nap or Night, which then sticks; the label is sent with every start and past sleep. A running sleep's label is the same toggle, and a tap saves it at once (`editSleep`, `endedAt` null). A finished sleep's sheet keeps its stored label when its start moves: the past never relabels itself.
- **P3: The wake.** The running sheet shows "Woke · Now · Change" above Wake up, so Wake up saves at the tap's time in two taps from Today. "Change" sets an earlier wake for a forgotten tap. A wake must come after she fell asleep: an earlier one reads "Pick a time after she fell asleep." and Wake up waits; a wake picked in the minute she fell asleep lands 1 ms after the start, which M3.1 accepts. A wake can't be undone, since M3.1 can't reopen a sleep, as a feed's Finish can't.
- **P4: Showing at once.** A start or a past sleep shows on Today at once (the card, the list, the last sleep) and goes back if refused; a wake waits for the household, as a feed's Finish does. An edit or delete shows at once on Today and every cached day, as M3.4's do. Today's Sleep row isn't shifted at once (its longest sleep can't be worked out from a shift); it follows the household's answer a moment later, while cached days total their sleeps again at once.
- **P5: A past sleep** opens on the hour before now, or at Log at's slot for an hour unless that runs past now. "Fell asleep" and "Woke" are the sheets' date-and-time fields; a sleep lasts a day at most ("A sleep can last a day at most."), and Save waits for a wake after the start.
- **P6: A finished sleep's sheet** ("Edit sleep") opens from Today's list and History's details, keeps the exact stored times while their fields are left alone (M3.4's rule), saves the whole sleep through `PATCH /api/sleeps/:id`, and deletes after asking, with Undo. A running sleep opens the Sleep sheet instead, from both places.
- **P7: Refusals.** "{Name} started a sleep at 9:10 AM." settles a start with OK, and a held start that meets it isn't kept. The sheet turns to her running sleep, with its "Woke" time and Wake up under the note: that's §5's "Wake at …" with the stop-time correction, and closing the sheet is its Cancel. "This overlaps the nap at 1:00–2:30 PM." (or "the night at 9:20 PM–now") waits for a changed time. "{Name} changed this sleep a moment ago." offers "Wake anyway", "Change it anyway" or "Delete anyway" while the tap still fits her sleep as it is now; on the edit sheet it's M3.4's "Save mine anyway" or "Use {Name}'s". A past sleep saved before with other values offers "Save my changes".
- **P8: Toasts:** "Sleep started", "Sleep saved" and "Sleep deleted", each with Undo for five seconds; "Sleep restored" from Recently deleted, with Undo; nothing after a wake.
- **P9: The Day grid.** The sleep column is 44 px (`left-14 w-11`) between the hours and the feeds, which now start at 104 px (`left-26`). A sleep block is a neutral `muted-foreground` fill with its name and a compact length: "Nap / 1h10", "Night / 10h30", "45m", or "now" while it runs; its accessible name is the full "Nap at 7:10 AM: 1 h 10 min, Woke 8:20 AM". A night is cut where the day begins and ends, as a feed is. The day's totals gain "11 h 20 min asleep · 2 h 50 min naps · 8 h 30 min night".
- **P10: The Week view.** A stripe at each column's left (`left-0.5 w-[10%]`, `data-kind="sleep"`), the bars moving to `left-[15%] w-[43%]` and the dots to 60 % and 80 %; the legend gains "Sleep"; each column's name gains "6 h 10 min asleep, 6 h 10 min night".
- **P11: Trends' Sleep chart.** Hours on the axis (at least 6 h tall); Naps in the full accent and Night in the lighter shade, as Left and Right are; its numbers "Naps 3 h 5 min · Night 10 h 5 min · Longest 6 h 10 min". On Week and Month the longest is the average of each day's longest, like every number there.
- **P12: Recently deleted.** A sleep's row says "Nap" or "Night" with how long she slept, or "Running when deleted". A sleep still running when it was deleted can't come back over a sleep started since: "It was still running when it was deleted, so it would run on over the sleep Emily started at 9:10 AM. It can't come back."
- **P13: History's chips** are Feeds, Diapers, Sleep, Growth: 313.5 px wide at 360 px, so they fit until M3.3's fifth, which M3.3 wraps.
- **P14: Size.** The replay measured +4.47 kB gzip of JS (325.81 to 330.28 kB of `bundle:check`'s 350) and +0.09 kB of CSS (11.89 to 11.98 kB of 15) against `d9f5e2c`, built with `CLOUDFLARE_ENV=local`, and the Worker +0.03 KiB gzip (260.73 to 260.76 of 400).

## Review Focus

The five ways this is most likely to fail a parent at 3 AM, each with the test that pins it:

1. **A forgotten Wake up.** The timer keeps counting, and "Change" wakes her at the time she really woke, never before she fell asleep.
   - Pinned by `e2e/sleep.spec.ts` ("wakes her at the time she woke when the tap comes later, but not before she fell asleep", Task 4) and `apps/web/test/sleep-sheet.test.ts` ("is now, unless a failed wake is held…", Task 4).
2. **The other phone got there first.** Two parents start a sleep, or one edits while the other woke her.
   - Pinned by `e2e/kept-entries.spec.ts` ("lets a sleep's start go for good once it meets the other phone's running sleep", Task 4), `e2e/sleep-edit.spec.ts` ("says who changed the sleep first, and saves mine on top of theirs when asked", Task 5) and `apps/web/test/recovery.test.ts` ("a sleep the other phone got to first", Task 2).
3. **No connection.** A start tapped with no signal is kept on the phone through a reload, and asked about before it's sent again under its actionId.
   - Pinned by `e2e/kept-entries.spec.ts` ("keeps a sleep started with no connection through a reload…", Task 4).
4. **A night across midnight.** It's on both days: the Day grid cuts it at midnight, the Week view draws a stripe in both columns, and each day counts only its own part.
   - Pinned by `apps/web/test/history-model.test.ts` ("draws each sleep in a column of its own, cut where the day begins…", Task 6), `history-week.test.ts` ("draws a night across midnight as a stripe in both days…", Task 7), `e2e/history-week.spec.ts` (Task 7) and the domain's running sleep ("starts with the part of last night's sleep after midnight", Task 8).
5. **A small phone.** Four quick actions, each a 44 px target, and four chips fit 360 px without scrolling sideways.
   - Pinned by `e2e/today.spec.ts` ("fits all four quick actions on a 360-pixel-wide phone…", Task 4) and `e2e/history-sleep.spec.ts` ("fits all four chips on a 360-pixel-wide phone…", Task 6).

### Task 1: A sleep in Today's last 24 hours, and in every entry helper

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/contracts/src/today.ts`, `apps/worker/src/core/today.ts`, `docs/api.json` (generated)
- Create: `apps/web/src/features/today/sleep-parts.ts`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/api/today-change.ts`, `apps/web/src/api/swap.ts`, `apps/web/src/features/edit/stale.ts`, `apps/web/src/features/edit/stale-note.tsx`, `apps/web/src/features/history/entry-labels.ts`, `apps/web/src/features/today/kind-icons.ts`, `apps/web/src/features/today/recent-rows.ts`
- Test: `packages/contracts/test/today.test.ts`, `apps/worker/test/today-core.test.ts`, `apps/web/test/today-rows.test.ts`, `apps/web/test/history-labels.test.ts`, `apps/web/test/entry-swaps.test.ts`, `apps/web/test/optimistic.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: M3.1's `Sleep`, `SleepKind` (`@bt/contracts`), `sleepElapsed`, `sleepTouches` (`@bt/domain`), `liveSleepsSince` (`core/sleeps/reads.ts`), `sleptAt` (`test/today-fixtures.ts`)
- Produces:
  - `RecentEntry` gains `{ kind: "sleep"; sleep: Sleep }`, which an app without it skips. `todayOf(sql, now).recent` lists the sleeps that started in the last 24 hours, by `startedAt`, newest first, a running one included.
  - `api/today-change.ts`: `recordOf(entry: RecentEntry): Feed | Diaper | Sleep`; `idOf`, `timeOf` (a sleep's `startedAt`), `versionOf` read through it; `changeOf` gives `NOTHING` for a sleep, so Today's totals aren't shifted by one (P4).
  - `api/swap.ts`: `dayCopy` finds a cached day's sleep, `todayCopy` Today's running sleep, and `swappedDay` swaps a sleep and totals the day with its sleeps.
  - `features/today/sleep-parts.ts`: `SLEEP_TITLES: Record<SleepKind, "Nap" | "Night">`, `sleepTitle(sleep)`, `sleepParts(sleep, timeZone, now, locale?)`, giving `["Asleep now", "42 min"]` or `["1 h 20 min", "Woke 3:30 AM"]`.
  - `KIND_ICONS.sleep` (`MoonStarIcon`); `RowKind` gains `"sleep"`; `recentRows` gives a sleep's row with `opens: null` (Task 5 opens it).
  - `entryName(entry: RecentEntry, display, now)` names a sleep: "Nap at 1:10 AM: 1 h 20 min, Woke 2:30 AM", or "Night at 8:48 AM: Asleep now, 42 min".
  - `features/edit/stale.ts` and `stale-note.tsx` read a record through `recordOf`; `stale.ts`'s own `recordIn` is gone.

```ts
import type { Sleep, SleepKind } from "@bt/contracts"
import { sleepElapsed } from "@bt/domain"
import { clockTime } from "../../clock-time.ts"
import { formatMinutes } from "./format.ts"

export const SLEEP_TITLES = { nap: "Nap", night: "Night" } as const satisfies Record<SleepKind, string>

export const sleepTitle = ({ kind }: Sleep) => SLEEP_TITLES[kind]

export const sleepParts = (sleep: Sleep, timeZone: string, now: number, locale?: string) => {
  const slept = formatMinutes(sleepElapsed(sleep, now))
  if (sleep.endedAt === null) return ["Asleep now", slept]
  return [slept, `Woke ${clockTime(sleep.endedAt, timeZone, locale)}`]
}
```

```diff
+    case "diaper":
+      return entry.diaper.occurredAt
+    case "sleep":

+  }
+}
+
+export const versionOf = (entry: RecentEntry) => recordOf(entry).version
 
 export const changeOf = (entry: RecentEntry | null): Change => {
-  if (entry === null) return NOTHING
+  if (entry === null || entry.kind === "sleep") return NOTHING
   return entry.kind === "feed" ? { feeds: [entry.feed], diapers: [] } : { feeds: [], diapers: [entry.diaper] }
 }
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Run the tests and the gates.** The Step 2 command: PASS. Then `pnpm typecheck`, `pnpm lint` and `pnpm test`: 3,457 tests in 208 files.

- [ ] **Step 5: Commit.** `git add -A`, then `git commit -m "Today's last 24 hours list sleeps by their start, as a nap or a night with how long she slept and who logged it, and every entry helper knows a sleep"`.

### Task 2: A sleep's actions: sent, shown at once, kept, explained and undone

**Files:**
- Modify: `apps/web/src/api/actions.ts`, `send.ts`, `pending.ts`, `today-change.ts`, `today-cache.ts`, `swap.ts`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/sheets/sending.ts`, `sheets-state.ts`, `use-reopen.ts`, `held.ts`, `failure.ts`, `recovery.ts`, `redo.ts`, `save-changes.ts`, `undo.ts`
- Test: `apps/web/test/send.test.ts`, `today-cache.test.ts`, `entry-swaps.test.ts`, `sheets-state.test.ts`, `failure.test.ts`, `recovery.test.ts`, `restore-toast.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1's `recordOf`, `idOf`, `timeOf`, `dayCopy`, `todayCopy`; M3.1's `NewSleep`, `SleepStart`, `SleepEdit`, `SavedSleep`, `SleepConflict`, `client.sleeps.*`
- Produces:
  - `api/actions.ts`: `SleepAction` = `logSleep` (`NewSleep`), `startSleep` (`SleepStart`), `wakeSleep` (`TimedAction`), `editSleep` (`SleepEdit`), `deleteSleep` and `restoreSleep` (`VersionedAction`), each with `id` but the first two; `isSleepAction`. `Action` gains them, which a kept entry stored before reads as before.
  - `api/send.ts`: `send` routes them to M3.1's six endpoints; `Saved` gains `SavedSleep`.
  - `api/pending.ts`: `pendingSleep(NewSleep, author): Sleep` and `startedSleep(SleepStart, author): Sleep`, under `pendingId(actionId)`.
  - `api/today-change.ts`: `later<Logged extends { id; startedAt }>(current, record)`, for feeds and sleeps.
  - `api/today-cache.ts`: `pendingToday` shows a start or a past sleep at once in `activeSleep`, `lastSleep` and `recent`; `withdrawnToday` puts them back; `savedToday` swaps in the household's sleep, clears `activeSleep` once she wakes or it's deleted.
  - `api/swap.ts`: `Swapping` gains `editSleep` and `deleteSleep`; `swappedEntry` edits a sleep's label and times; `swappedToday` sets `activeSleep` and `lastSleep` after a sleep's edit or delete.
  - `sheets/sending.ts`: `SheetKind` gains `"sleep"`, `"pastSleep"` and `"editSleep"` (an edit sheet); `logSleep`, `startSleep`, `wakeSleep` and `deleteSleep` close their sheet on send.
  - `sheets/sheets-state.ts`: `logSleep` and `startSleep` are new entries, kept on the phone until confirmed (M5.6). `use-reopen.ts`: `sleep` and `pastSleep` reopen once Today loads.
  - `sheets/held.ts`: `SLEEP_ALREADY_ACTIVE` settles a held start without asking.
  - `sheets/failure.ts`: a sleep conflict's sentence (P7); on the sleep sheets, `end_not_after_start` reads "Pick a time after she fell asleep." and `out_of_range` "A sleep can last a day at most."
  - `sheets/redo.ts`: `redoneSleep(sending, sleep)`; `save-changes.ts`: `sleepReplay(sending, sleep)`; `recovery.ts` routes `SleepConflict`, and OK settles `SLEEP_ALREADY_ACTIVE`.
  - `sheets/undo.ts`: "Sleep started", "Sleep saved", "Sleep deleted" with Undo; `restoredToast` for a sleep ("Sleep restored"); nothing for a wake.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.** `git diff 774f68d^ 774f68d -- <path> | git apply -3` for each test file below. `sheets-state.test.ts`'s table lists every action kind, so it names the six sleep sends.

```diff
 const ACTION_ID = "4b1c2a5e-8f3d-4c6b-9a7e-2d1f0e3c5b6a"
```

```diff
+
+describe("a sleep on Today", () => {
+  const EARLIER = sleptAt(NOW - 5 * HOUR)
+  const BEFORE = todayWith({ lastSleep: EARLIER, recent: [{ kind: "sleep", sleep: EARLIER }] })
+  const START = {
+    kind: "startSleep" as const,
+    payload: { actionId: ACTION_ID, kind: "nap" as const, startedAt: NOW - 5 * MINUTE },
+  }
+  const PAST = {
+    kind: "logSleep" as const,
+    payload: { actionId: ACTION_ID, kind: "night" as const, startedAt: NOW - 3 * HOUR, endedAt: NOW - HOUR },
+  }
+  const STARTED = {
+    id: pendingId(ACTION_ID),
+    kind: "nap" as const,
+    startedAt: NOW - 5 * MINUTE,
+    endedAt: null,
+    version: 1,
+    createdBy: ANDREW,
+    createdAt: NOW,
+    updatedBy: ANDREW,
+    updatedAt: NOW,
+    deletedAt: null,
+  }
+
+  it("shows a sleep being started as the running and the last sleep, first on the list, leaving the totals to the household", () => {
+    expect(pendingToday(BEFORE, START, pending)).toEqual({
+      ...BEFORE,
+      activeSleep: STARTED,
+      lastSleep: STARTED,
+      recent: [
+        { kind: "sleep", sleep: STARTED },
+        { kind: "sleep", sleep: EARLIER },
+      ],
+    })
+  })
+
+  it("lists a past sleep and makes it the last sleep when it's the latest, without running it", () => {
+    const next = pendingToday(BEFORE, PAST, pending)
+    expect(next.activeSleep).toBeNull()
+    expect(next.lastSleep).toMatchObject({ id: pendingId(ACTION_ID), kind: "night", endedAt: NOW - HOUR })
+    expect(next.recent.map((entry) => entry.kind === "sleep" && entry.sleep.id)).toEqual([
+      pendingId(ACTION_ID),
+      EARLIER.id,
+    ])
+    expect(next.totals).toEqual(NO_TOTALS)
+  })
+
+  it("puts the running and the last sleep back when a start or a past sleep fails", () => {
+    for (const action of [START, PAST]) {
+      expect(withdrawnToday(pendingToday(BEFORE, action, pending), action, pending, BEFORE)).toEqual(BEFORE)
+    }
+  })
+
+  it("swaps a sleep being started for the household's answer in the running, last and recent slots", () => {
+    const saved = { ...STARTED, id: "sleep-1" }
+    const next = savedToday(pendingToday(BEFORE, START, pending), { sleep: saved, changed: [] }, START, NOW)
+    expect(next).toEqual({
+      ...BEFORE,
+      activeSleep: saved,
+      lastSleep: saved,
+      recent: [
+        { kind: "sleep", sleep: saved },
+        { kind: "sleep", sleep: EARLIER },
+      ],
+    })
+  })
+
+  it("clears the running sleep once she wakes, and keeps it as the last sleep", () => {
+    const running = sleptAt(NOW - 40 * MINUTE, { endedAt: null })
+    const woken = { ...running, endedAt: NOW, version: 2 }
+    const today = todayWith({ activeSleep: running, lastSleep: running, recent: [{ kind: "sleep", sleep: running }] })
+    const wake = {
+      kind: "wakeSleep" as const,
+      id: running.id,
+      payload: { actionId: ACTION_ID, version: 1, occurredAt: NOW },
+    }
+    expect(savedToday(today, { sleep: woken, changed: [] }, wake, NOW)).toEqual({
+      ...today,
+      activeSleep: null,
+      lastSleep: woken,
+      recent: [{ kind: "sleep", sleep: woken }],
+    })
+  })
+
+  it("drops a sleep the household answers as deleted from the list and the running slot", () => {
+    const running = sleptAt(NOW - 40 * MINUTE, { endedAt: null })
+    const today = todayWith({ activeSleep: running, lastSleep: running, recent: [{ kind: "sleep", sleep: running }] })
+    const remove = { kind: "deleteSleep" as const, id: running.id, payload: { actionId: ACTION_ID, version: 1 } }
+    const next = savedToday(today, { sleep: { ...running, deletedAt: NOW, version: 2 }, changed: [] }, remove, NOW)
+    expect([next.activeSleep, next.recent]).toEqual([null, []])
+  })
+})
```

```diff
+    const edit: Action = {
+      kind: "editSleep",
+      id: NAP.id,
+      payload: { actionId: ACTION_ID, version: 1, kind: "night", startedAt: local("01:00"), endedAt: local("02:30") },
+    }
+    const pended = await pendIn(queryClient, edit, NOW_ON_DAY)
+    const edited = {
+      ...NAP,
+      kind: "night" as const,
+      startedAt: local("01:00"),
+      updatedBy: ANDREW,
+      updatedAt: NOW_ON_DAY,
+    }
+    expect(queryClient.getQueryData<Today>(queryKeys.today)?.recent).toContainEqual({ kind: "sleep", sleep: edited })
+    expect(queryClient.getQueryData<DayPeriod>(queryKeys.day(DAY))).toMatchObject({
+      sleeps: [edited, RUNNING_SLEEP],
+      totals: totalsIn(BOUNDS, { feeds: [], diapers: [], sleeps: [edited, RUNNING_SLEEP] }, NOW_ON_DAY),
+    })
+    rollBack(queryClient, edit, pended)
+    expect(queryClient.getQueryData<DayPeriod>(queryKeys.day(DAY))?.sleeps).toEqual(before?.sleeps)
+  })
+
+  it("keeps a running sleep running through an edit of its start", async () => {
+    const queryClient = cached()
+    const edit: Action = {
+      kind: "editSleep",
+      id: RUNNING_SLEEP.id,
+      payload: { actionId: ACTION_ID, version: 1, kind: "nap", startedAt: local("08:30"), endedAt: null },
+    }
+    await pendIn(queryClient, edit, NOW_ON_DAY)
+    const today = queryClient.getQueryData<Today>(queryKeys.today)
+    expect([today?.activeSleep?.startedAt, today?.lastSleep?.startedAt]).toEqual([local("08:30"), local("08:30")])
+  })
+
+  it("takes a deleted running sleep off Today and the cached day at once, the nap before it the last sleep again", async () => {
+    const queryClient = cached()
+    const remove: Action = {
+      kind: "deleteSleep",
+      id: RUNNING_SLEEP.id,
+      payload: { actionId: ACTION_ID, version: 1 },
+    }
+    await pendIn(queryClient, remove, NOW_ON_DAY)
+    const today = queryClient.getQueryData<Today>(queryKeys.today)
+    expect([today?.activeSleep, today?.lastSleep]).toEqual([null, NAP])
+    expect(queryClient.getQueryData<DayPeriod>(queryKeys.day(DAY))?.sleeps).toEqual([NAP])
+  })
+})
```

```diff
 const ACTION_ID = "4b1c2a5e-8f3d-4c6b-9a7e-2d1f0e3c5b6a"
 const FRESH = "9d2e1f3c-5b4a-4e6d-8c7b-1a0f2e3d4c5b"
@@ -418,3 +425,82 @@ describe("a measurement the other phone got to first", () => {
     expect(recoveryOf({ sending: edited, error: gone })).toBeNull()
   })
 })
+
+describe("a sleep the other phone got to first", () => {
+  const RUNNING = sleptAt(STARTED, { id: "sleep-1", endedAt: null, version: 3 })
+  const sleepConflict = (sending: Sending, code: SleepConflict["code"], current = RUNNING): Failed => ({
+    sending,
+    error: new SleepConflict({ code, current }),
+  })
+  const WAKE: Sending = {
+    sheet: "sleep",
+    action: { kind: "wakeSleep", id: "sleep-1", payload: MINE },
+  }
+  const MOVED_START: Sending = {
+    sheet: "sleep",
+    action: {
+      kind: "editSleep",
+      id: "sleep-1",
+      payload: { actionId: ACTION_ID, version: 2, kind: "nap", startedAt: STARTED - 5 * MINUTE, endedAt: null },
+    },
+  }
+  const REMOVE: Sending = {
+    sheet: "sleep",
+    action: { kind: "deleteSleep", id: "sleep-1", payload: { actionId: ACTION_ID, version: 2 } },
+  }
+  const PAST: Sending = {
+    sheet: "pastSleep",
+    action: {
+      kind: "logSleep",
+      payload: { actionId: ACTION_ID, kind: "night", startedAt: STARTED, endedAt: NOW },
+    },
+  }
+  const START: Sending = {
+    sheet: "sleep",
+    action: { kind: "startSleep", payload: { actionId: ACTION_ID, kind: "nap", startedAt: NOW } },
+  }
+
+  it("wakes her anyway on their version while the sleep still runs, from before the tap", () => {
+    const recovery = recoveryOf(sleepConflict(WAKE, "STALE_VERSION"))
+    expect(recovery?.label).toBe("Wake anyway")
+    expect(recovery?.sendingAt(TAP)).toEqual({ ...WAKE, action: { ...WAKE.action, payload: { ...MINE, version: 3 } } })
+  })
+
+  it("offers nothing more once they've woken her or deleted it, so OK settles it", () => {
+    const woken = { ...RUNNING, endedAt: NOW - MINUTE }
+    expect(recoveryOf(sleepConflict(WAKE, "STALE_VERSION", woken))).toBeNull()
+    expect(recoveryOf(sleepConflict(REMOVE, "STALE_VERSION", { ...RUNNING, deletedAt: NOW }))).toBeNull()
+    expect(offersOk(sleepConflict(WAKE, "STALE_VERSION", woken))).toBe(true)
+  })
+
+  it("changes a running sleep's start, or deletes it, anyway on their version", () => {
+    expect(recoveryOf(sleepConflict(MOVED_START, "STALE_VERSION"))?.label).toBe("Change it anyway")
+    expect(recoveryOf(sleepConflict(REMOVE, "STALE_VERSION"))?.label).toBe("Delete anyway")
+  })
+
+  it("saves a past sleep's changes as an edit of the saved one, under a fresh actionId", () => {
+    const saved = sleptAt(STARTED, { id: "sleep-2", kind: "nap", endedAt: NOW - MINUTE, version: 1 })
+    const recovery = recoveryOf(sleepConflict(PAST, "ACTION_REPLAY_MISMATCH", saved))
+    expect(recovery?.label).toBe("Save my changes")
+    expect(recovery?.sendingAt(TAP)).toEqual({
+      sheet: "pastSleep",
+      action: {
+        kind: "editSleep",
+        id: "sleep-2",
+        payload: { actionId: FRESH, version: 1, kind: "night", startedAt: STARTED, endedAt: NOW },
+      },
+    })
+  })
+
+  it("settles a start that meets their running sleep with OK, without asking to keep it", () => {
+    const failed = sleepConflict(START, "SLEEP_ALREADY_ACTIVE")
+    expect(recoveryOf(failed)).toBeNull()
+    expect(offersOk(failed)).toBe(true)
+    expect(settlesWithoutAsking(failed.error)).toBe(true)
+  })
+
+  it("asks first after an overlap, and offers no way on but changing the times", () => {
+    const failed = sleepConflict(PAST, "SLEEP_OVERLAP")
+    expect([recoveryOf(failed), offersOk(failed), settlesWithoutAsking(failed.error)]).toEqual([null, false, false])
+  })
+})
```

```diff
@@ -1,15 +1,17 @@
-import type { SavedDiaper, SavedFeed, SavedMeasurement } from "@bt/contracts"
+import type { SavedDiaper, SavedFeed, SavedMeasurement, SavedSleep } from "@bt/contracts"
 import {
   type Action,
   type DiaperAction,
   type FeedAction,
   isDiaperAction,
   isMeasurementAction,
+  isSleepAction,
   type MeasurementAction,
+  type SleepAction,
 } from "./actions.ts"
 import type { HouseholdApi } from "./client.ts"
 
-export type Saved = SavedDiaper | SavedFeed | SavedMeasurement
+export type Saved = SavedDiaper | SavedFeed | SavedMeasurement | SavedSleep
 
 type OnFeed = Extract<FeedAction, { readonly id: string }>
 type NewFeed = Exclude<FeedAction, OnFeed>
@@ -96,7 +98,25 @@ const sendMeasurement = (api: HouseholdApi, action: MeasurementAction): Promise<
   }
 }
 
+const sendSleep = (api: HouseholdApi, action: SleepAction): Promise<SavedSleep> => {
+  switch (action.kind) {
+    case "logSleep":
+      return api.request((client) => client.sleeps.logSleep({ payload: action.payload }))
+    case "startSleep":
+      return api.request((client) => client.sleeps.startSleep({ payload: action.payload }))
+    case "wakeSleep":
+      return api.request((client) => client.sleeps.wakeSleep({ params: { id: action.id }, payload: action.payload }))
+    case "editSleep":
+      return api.request((client) => client.sleeps.editSleep({ params: { id: action.id }, payload: action.payload }))
+    case "deleteSleep":
+      return api.request((client) => client.sleeps.deleteSleep({ params: { id: action.id }, payload: action.payload }))
+    case "restoreSleep":
+      return api.request((client) => client.sleeps.restoreSleep({ params: { id: action.id }, payload: action.payload }))
+  }
+}
+
 export const send = (api: HouseholdApi, action: Action): Promise<Saved> => {
+  if (isSleepAction(action)) return sendSleep(api, action)
   if (isMeasurementAction(action)) return sendMeasurement(api, action)
   if (isDiaperAction(action)) return sendDiaper(api, action)
   return "id" in action ? sendOnFeed(api, action) : sendNewFeed(api, action)
```

```diff
-import type { Feed, Measurement, Side } from "@bt/contracts"
+import type { Feed, Measurement, Side, Sleep } from "@bt/contracts"

 import { SIDE_NAMES } from "../sides.ts"
 import type { Recovery, Sending } from "./sending.ts"
@@ -101,3 +101,21 @@ export const mineAnyway = ({ sheet, action }: Sending, current: Measurement): Re
   if (action.kind !== "editMeasurement" || current.deletedAt !== null) return null
   return { label: "Save mine anyway", sendingAt: () => ({ sheet, action: withVersion(action, current.version) }) }
 }
+
+const SLEEP_REDO_LABELS = new Map<Action["kind"], string>([
+  ["wakeSleep", "Wake anyway"],
+  ["editSleep", "Change it anyway"],
+  ["deleteSleep", "Delete anyway"],
+])
+
+const sleepFits = (action: Action, sleep: Sleep) => {
+  if (action.kind === "wakeSleep") return sleep.endedAt === null && action.payload.occurredAt > sleep.startedAt
+  if (action.kind === "editSleep") return sleep.endedAt === null || action.payload.endedAt !== null
+  return action.kind === "deleteSleep"
+}
+
+export const redoneSleep = ({ sheet, action }: Sending, sleep: Sleep): Recovery | null => {
+  const label = SLEEP_REDO_LABELS.get(action.kind)
+  if (label === undefined || sleep.deletedAt !== null || !sleepFits(action, sleep)) return null
+  return { label, sendingAt: () => ({ sheet, action: withVersion(action, sleep.version) }) }
+}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest"
import { sleepStatus } from "../src/features/today/sleep-status.ts"
import { MINUTE, NOW, sleptAt } from "./today-fixtures.ts"

describe("Today's sleep status", () => {
  it("counts a running sleep up to now", () => {
    const running = sleptAt(NOW - 42 * MINUTE, { endedAt: null })
    expect(sleepStatus({ activeSleep: running, lastSleep: running }, NOW)).toEqual({
      kind: "asleep",
      sleep: running,
      seconds: 42 * 60,
    })
  })

  it("counts the time since she woke from the last sleep", () => {
    const nap = sleptAt(NOW - 3 * 60 * MINUTE, { endedAt: NOW - 130 * MINUTE })
    expect(sleepStatus({ activeSleep: null, lastSleep: nap }, NOW)).toEqual({
      kind: "awake",
      sleep: nap,
      wokeAt: NOW - 130 * MINUTE,
      seconds: 130 * 60,
    })
  })

  it("says nothing yet before the first sleep", () => {
    expect(sleepStatus({ activeSleep: null, lastSleep: null }, NOW)).toEqual({ kind: "none" })
  })

  it("never counts below zero for a wake a phone logged ahead of the clock", () => {
    const ahead = sleptAt(NOW - 60 * MINUTE, { endedAt: NOW + 2 * MINUTE })
    expect(sleepStatus({ activeSleep: null, lastSleep: ahead }, NOW)).toMatchObject({ kind: "awake", seconds: 0 })
  })
})
```

```ts
import { expect, type Page, test } from "@playwright/test"
import {
  caregiversOf,
  MINUTE,
  NO_TOTALS,
  NOW,
  openToday,
  playHousehold,
  runningSleep,
  sleepingToday,
  todayWith,
} from "./played-household.ts"

test.use({ timezoneId: "Europe/London" })

const total = (page: Page, label: string) =>
  page.locator("dl > div").filter({ has: page.getByRole("term").filter({ hasText: label }) })

test("counts a running sleep up each second, a nap or a night, with when she fell asleep", async ({ page }) => {
  const { andrew } = await caregiversOf(page)
  await playHousehold(page, sleepingToday(runningSleep(andrew.id, NOW - 42 * MINUTE - 25_000, "night")))
  await openToday(page)
  await expect(page.getByText("Asleep now · Night")).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.getByRole("timer")).toHaveText("42:25")
  await expect(page.getByText(/Fell asleep 8:47\sAM/u)).toBeVisible()
  await page.clock.setFixedTime(NOW + 1_000)
  await expect(page.getByRole("timer")).toHaveText("42:26")
})

test("counts the time since she woke, with the sleep before it", async ({ page }) => {
  const { andrew } = await caregiversOf(page)
  const nap = { ...runningSleep(andrew.id, NOW - 3 * 60 * MINUTE), endedAt: NOW - 130 * MINUTE }
  await playHousehold(page, sleepingToday(nap))
  await openToday(page)
  await expect(page.getByText("Since she woke")).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.getByText("2 h 10 min")).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.getByText(/Woke 7:20\sAM · Nap 50 min/u)).toBeVisible()
})

test("says there's no sleep yet before the first one", async ({ page }) => {
  await playHousehold(page, todayWith())
  await openToday(page)
  await expect(page.getByText("No sleeps yet")).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.getByText("The first sleep you log will show here.")).toBeVisible()
})

test("totals the day's sleep, with naps and night under it", async ({ page }) => {
  const totals = { ...NO_TOTALS, sleepsStarted: 3, sleepSeconds: 40_800, napSeconds: 10_200, nightSeconds: 30_600 }
  await playHousehold(page, { ...todayWith(), totals })
  await openToday(page)
  await expect(total(page, "Sleep")).toContainText("11 h 20 min")
  await expect(total(page, "Sleep")).toContainText("Naps 2 h 50 min · Night 8 h 30 min")
})
```

```tsx
export const LiveDot = () => (
  <span aria-hidden="true" className="size-2 rounded-full bg-primary motion-safe:animate-pulse" />
)
```

```ts
import type { Sleep, Today } from "@bt/contracts"
import { sleepElapsed } from "@bt/domain"

export type SleepStatus =
  | { readonly kind: "asleep"; readonly sleep: Sleep; readonly seconds: number }
  | { readonly kind: "awake"; readonly sleep: Sleep; readonly wokeAt: number; readonly seconds: number }
  | { readonly kind: "none" }

const SECOND_MS = 1000

export const sleepStatus = ({ activeSleep, lastSleep }: Pick<Today, "activeSleep" | "lastSleep">, now: number) => {
  if (activeSleep !== null) {
    return { kind: "asleep", sleep: activeSleep, seconds: sleepElapsed(activeSleep, now) } satisfies SleepStatus
  }
  if (lastSleep === null || lastSleep.endedAt === null) return { kind: "none" } satisfies SleepStatus
  const wokeAt = lastSleep.endedAt
  const seconds = Math.max(0, Math.floor((now - wokeAt) / SECOND_MS))
  return { kind: "awake", sleep: lastSleep, wokeAt, seconds } satisfies SleepStatus
}
```

```tsx
import type { Today } from "@bt/contracts"
import { formatElapsed, sleepElapsed } from "@bt/domain"
import { clockTime } from "../../clock-time.ts"
import type { Display } from "./display.ts"
import { formatMinutes, formatSince } from "./format.ts"
import { LiveDot } from "./live-dot.tsx"
import { SLEEP_TITLES } from "./sleep-parts.ts"
import { sleepStatus } from "./sleep-status.ts"
import { StatusCard } from "./status-card.tsx"

interface SleepCardProps {
  readonly today: Today
  readonly display: Display
  readonly now: number
}

export const SleepCard = ({ today, display, now }: SleepCardProps) => {
  const status = sleepStatus(today, now)
  const at = (instant: number) => clockTime(instant, display.timeZone, display.locale)
  switch (status.kind) {
    case "asleep":
      return (
        <StatusCard
          label={
            <>
              <LiveDot />
              Asleep now · {SLEEP_TITLES[status.sleep.kind]}
            </>
          }
          value={formatElapsed(status.seconds)}
          detail={[`Fell asleep ${at(status.sleep.startedAt)}`]}
          running
        />
      )
    case "awake":
      return (
        <StatusCard
          label="Since she woke"
          value={formatSince(status.seconds)}
          detail={[
            `Woke ${at(status.wokeAt)}`,
            `${SLEEP_TITLES[status.sleep.kind]} ${formatMinutes(sleepElapsed(status.sleep, now))}`,
          ]}
        />
      )
    case "none":
      return <StatusCard label="No sleeps yet" detail={["The first sleep you log will show here."]} />
  }
}
```

### Task 4: The sleep sheet, from the bar's fourth button

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/features/sleep/sleep-state.ts`, `sleep-session.ts`, `sleep-kind-toggle.tsx`, `sleep-timer.tsx`, `sleep-start.tsx`, `start-sleep-view.tsx`, `running-sleep-view.tsx`, `sleep-sheet.tsx`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/sheets/when-field.tsx`, `apps/web/src/shell/log-sheets.tsx`, `apps/web/src/features/today/quick-actions.tsx`
- Test: create `apps/web/test/sleep-sheet.test.ts` and `e2e/sleep.spec.ts`; modify `e2e/today.spec.ts` and `e2e/kept-entries.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 2's `SleepAction`, `SheetKind` `"sleep"`, toasts and failure notes; Task 3's card and `e2e/played-household.ts` helpers; M1.12's `Sheet`, `WhenField`, `resolveWhen`, `whenAt`, `NOW`, `DeleteEntry`, `FailureNote`, `tapTime`, `useNow`; M3.1's `sleepKindAt`, `SLEEP_KINDS`
- Produces:
  - `features/sleep/sleep-state.ts`: `sleepState(activeSleep)`, one of `idle`, `starting` (a start still being saved) or `running`; `sleepWaits(busy)`; `startAction(actionId, kind, startedAt)`, `wakeAction(sleep, occurredAt)`, `runningEdit(sleep, kind, startedAt)` (`endedAt` null) and `deleteAction(sleep)`, each with the sleep's version and a fresh actionId where it's not given.
  - `features/sleep/sleep-session.ts`: `AFTER_SLEEP` ("Pick a time after she fell asleep."); `sleepStartSession(failed, zone)`, which keeps a failed start's actionId, time and label; `kindShown(picked, start, zone, now)` (P2); `sentWake(failed, zone)`.
  - `SleepKindToggle({ kind, disabled?, onChange })`: "Nap or night", Nap | Night.
  - `SleepSheet`, mounted in `LogSheets`: Start sleep, or the running sleep's timer, "Fell asleep … Change", its label, "Woke · Now · Change", Wake up and Delete sleep.
  - `WhenField` gains `tooEarly?: string`, the words for a time before `earliest`.
  - `QuickActions`: Bottle, Nursing, Diaper and Sleep, each icon stacked over its label (`flex-col gap-0.5 px-1 text-xs`).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.** `git checkout 4da7557 -- apps/web/test/sleep-sheet.test.ts e2e/sleep.spec.ts`, then `git diff 4da7557^ 4da7557 -- <path> | git apply -3` for `e2e/today.spec.ts` and `e2e/kept-entries.spec.ts`.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest"
import { Offline } from "../src/api/errors.ts"
import { pendingId } from "../src/api/pending.ts"
import { kindShown, sentWake, sleepStartSession } from "../src/features/sleep/sleep-session.ts"
import {
  deleteAction,
  runningEdit,
  sleepState,
  sleepWaits,
  startAction,
  wakeAction,
} from "../src/features/sleep/sleep-state.ts"
import type { Sending } from "../src/sheets/sending.ts"
import { NOW as NOW_WHEN, whenAt } from "../src/sheets/when.ts"
import { MINUTE, NOW, sleptAt, ZONE } from "./today-fixtures.ts"

const ACTION_ID = "4b1c2a5e-8f3d-4c6b-9a7e-2d1f0e3c5b6a"
const UUID = expect.stringMatching(/^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/u)
const SEVEN_THIRTY_PM = Date.UTC(2026, 9, 10, 2, 30)
const TWO_PM = Date.UTC(2026, 9, 9, 21)
const RUNNING = sleptAt(NOW - 40 * MINUTE, { id: "sleep-1", endedAt: null, version: 3 })

describe("the sleep sheet's state", () => {
  it("starts a sleep when none runs, waits on one being started, and controls a running one", () => {
    expect(sleepState(null)).toEqual({ kind: "idle" })
    expect(sleepState({ ...RUNNING, id: pendingId(ACTION_ID) })).toEqual({ kind: "starting" })
    expect(sleepState(RUNNING)).toEqual({ kind: "running", sleep: RUNNING })
  })

  it("holds the controls while the sleep sheet sends", () => {
    const sending: Sending = { sheet: "sleep", action: wakeAction(RUNNING, NOW) }
    expect([sleepWaits(sending), sleepWaits({ ...sending, sheet: "nursing" }), sleepWaits(null)]).toEqual([
      true,
      false,
      false,
    ])
  })

  it("starts, wakes, edits and deletes with the sleep's version and a fresh actionId", () => {
    expect(startAction(ACTION_ID, "night", NOW)).toEqual({
      kind: "startSleep",
      payload: { actionId: ACTION_ID, kind: "night", startedAt: NOW },
    })
    expect(wakeAction(RUNNING, NOW)).toEqual({
      kind: "wakeSleep",
      id: "sleep-1",
      payload: { actionId: UUID, version: 3, occurredAt: NOW },
    })
    expect(runningEdit(RUNNING, "night", NOW - 50 * MINUTE)).toEqual({
      kind: "editSleep",
      id: "sleep-1",
      payload: { actionId: UUID, version: 3, kind: "night", startedAt: NOW - 50 * MINUTE, endedAt: null },
    })
    expect(deleteAction(RUNNING)).toEqual({
      kind: "deleteSleep",
      id: "sleep-1",
      payload: { actionId: UUID, version: 3 },
    })
  })
})

describe("a sleep being started", () => {
  it("starts now, with a fresh actionId and the label left to the clock", () => {
    expect(sleepStartSession(null, ZONE)).toEqual({ actionId: UUID, when: NOW_WHEN, picked: null })
  })

  it("comes back as it was sent after a failure, so its retry sends the same start", () => {
    const failed = {
      sending: { sheet: "sleep" as const, action: startAction(ACTION_ID, "nap", TWO_PM) },
      error: new Offline(),
    }
    expect(sleepStartSession(failed, ZONE)).toEqual({ actionId: ACTION_ID, when: whenAt(TWO_PM, ZONE), picked: "nap" })
  })

  it("is a night from 7 PM to 7 AM on the household's clock and a nap otherwise, until a parent picks one", () => {
    const at = (instant: number) => ({ ok: true as const, instant })
    expect(kindShown(null, at(SEVEN_THIRTY_PM), ZONE, NOW)).toBe("night")
    expect(kindShown(null, at(TWO_PM), ZONE, NOW)).toBe("nap")
    expect(kindShown("nap", at(SEVEN_THIRTY_PM), ZONE, NOW)).toBe("nap")
  })

  it("follows the moment it's shown while the start can't be read yet", () => {
    expect(kindShown(null, { ok: false, issue: "unreadable" }, ZONE, SEVEN_THIRTY_PM)).toBe("night")
  })
})

describe("a wake", () => {
  it("is now, unless a failed wake is held, which keeps the time it sent", () => {
    const failed = {
      sending: { sheet: "sleep" as const, action: wakeAction(RUNNING, NOW - MINUTE) },
      error: new Offline(),
    }
    expect(sentWake(null, ZONE)).toEqual(NOW_WHEN)
    expect(sentWake(failed, ZONE)).toEqual(whenAt(NOW - MINUTE, ZONE))
  })
})
```

```ts
import { expect, type Page, test } from "@playwright/test"
import {
  caregiversOf,
  MINUTE,
  NOW,
  openToday,
  playHousehold,
  runningSleep,
  sleepingToday,
  todayWith,
} from "./played-household.ts"

const ACTION_ID = expect.stringMatching(/^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/u)
const FELL_ASLEEP = NOW - 42 * MINUTE

test.use({ timezoneId: "Europe/London" })

const openSleep = async (page: Page) => {
  await page.getByRole("region", { name: "Log", exact: true }).getByRole("button", { name: "Sleep" }).click()
  return page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "Sleep" })
}

test("starts a sleep in two taps, a nap by the household's clock at 9:30 AM", async ({ page }) => {
  const { andrew } = await caregiversOf(page)
  const { played, answer } = await playHousehold(page, todayWith())
  await answer("**/api/sleeps/start", () => {
    const sleep = runningSleep(andrew.id, NOW)
    played.today = sleepingToday(sleep)
    return { status: 201, json: { sleep, changed: [["today"]] } }
  })
  await openToday(page)
  await expect(page.getByText("No sleeps yet")).toBeVisible()
  const sheet = await openSleep(page)
  await expect(sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Nap", exact: true })).toHaveAttribute("aria-pressed", "true")
  await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Start sleep" }).click()
  await expect
    .poll(() => played.sent)
    .toEqual([
      { method: "POST", path: "/api/sleeps/start", body: { actionId: ACTION_ID, kind: "nap", startedAt: NOW } },
    ])
  await expect(sheet).toBeHidden()
  await expect(page.getByText("Asleep now · Nap")).toBeVisible()
})

test("wakes her in two taps, at the tap's time and the sleep's version, then counts the time since", async ({
  page,
}) => {
  const { andrew } = await caregiversOf(page)
  const sleep = runningSleep(andrew.id, FELL_ASLEEP, "nap", 2)
  const { played, answer } = await playHousehold(page, sleepingToday(sleep))
  await answer("**/api/sleeps/sleep-1/wake", ({ body }) => {
    const woken = { ...sleep, endedAt: body.occurredAt ?? NOW, version: 3 }
    played.today = sleepingToday(woken)
    return { status: 200, json: { sleep: woken, changed: [["today"]] } }
  })
  await openToday(page)
  await expect(page.getByRole("timer")).toHaveText("42:00")
  const sheet = await openSleep(page)
  await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Wake up" }).click()
  await expect
    .poll(() => played.sent)
    .toEqual([
      {
        method: "POST",
        path: "/api/sleeps/sleep-1/wake",
        body: { actionId: ACTION_ID, version: 2, occurredAt: NOW },
      },
    ])
  await expect(page.getByText("Since she woke")).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.getByText(/Nap 42 min/u)).toBeVisible()
})

test("wakes her at the time she woke when the tap comes later, but not before she fell asleep", async ({ page }) => {
  const { andrew } = await caregiversOf(page)
  const sleep = runningSleep(andrew.id, FELL_ASLEEP, "nap", 2)
  const { played, answer } = await playHousehold(page, sleepingToday(sleep))
  await answer("**/api/sleeps/sleep-1/wake", ({ body }) => {
    const woken = { ...sleep, endedAt: body.occurredAt ?? NOW, version: 3 }
    played.today = sleepingToday(woken)
    return { status: 200, json: { sleep: woken, changed: [["today"]] } }
  })
  await openToday(page)
  const sheet = await openSleep(page)
  await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Change" }).last().click()
  await sheet.getByLabel("Woke").fill("2026-10-09T08:40")
  await expect(sheet.getByText("Pick a time after she fell asleep.")).toBeVisible()
  await expect(sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Wake up" })).toBeDisabled()
  await sheet.getByLabel("Woke").fill("2026-10-09T09:10")
  await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Wake up" }).click()
  await expect.poll(() => played.sent.map(({ body }) => body.occurredAt)).toEqual([Date.UTC(2026, 9, 9, 16, 10)])
  await expect(page.getByText("Since she woke")).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.getByText("20 min")).toBeVisible()
})

test("calls a sleep that fell at 6:30 AM a night, until a parent picks nap", async ({ page }) => {
  const { andrew } = await caregiversOf(page)
  const { played, answer } = await playHousehold(page, todayWith())
  await answer("**/api/sleeps/start", ({ body }) => {
    const sleep = runningSleep(andrew.id, body.startedAt ?? NOW, body.kind ?? "nap")
    played.today = sleepingToday(sleep)
    return { status: 201, json: { sleep, changed: [["today"]] } }
  })
  await openToday(page)
  const sheet = await openSleep(page)
  await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Change" }).click()
  await sheet.getByLabel("Fell asleep").fill("2026-10-09T06:30")
  await expect(sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Night", exact: true })).toHaveAttribute("aria-pressed", "true")
  await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Nap", exact: true }).click()
  await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Start sleep" }).click()
  await expect
    .poll(() => played.sent.map(({ body }) => [body.kind, body.startedAt]))
    .toEqual([["nap", Date.UTC(2026, 9, 9, 13, 30)]])
})

test("moves a running sleep's start, and changes it to a night, each with its version", async ({ page }) => {
  const { andrew } = await caregiversOf(page)
  let sleep = runningSleep(andrew.id, FELL_ASLEEP, "nap", 2)
  const { played, answer } = await playHousehold(page, sleepingToday(sleep))
  await answer("**/api/sleeps/sleep-1", ({ body }) => {
    sleep = {
      ...sleep,
      kind: body.kind ?? sleep.kind,
      startedAt: body.startedAt ?? sleep.startedAt,
      version: sleep.version + 1,
    }
    played.today = sleepingToday(sleep)
    return { status: 200, json: { sleep, changed: [["today"]] } }
  })
  await openToday(page)
  const sheet = await openSleep(page)
  await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Night", exact: true }).click()
  await expect(sheet.getByText("Asleep · Night")).toBeVisible()
  await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Change" }).first().click()
  await sheet.getByLabel("Fell asleep").fill("2026-10-09T08:30")
  await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Save start" }).click()
  await expect
    .poll(() => played.sent.map(({ method, body }) => [method, body.version, body.kind, body.startedAt, body.endedAt]))
    .toEqual([
      ["PATCH", 2, "night", FELL_ASLEEP, null],
      ["PATCH", 3, "night", Date.UTC(2026, 9, 9, 15, 30), null],
    ])
})

test("deletes a running sleep after asking, and offers Undo", async ({ page }) => {
  const { andrew } = await caregiversOf(page)
  const sleep = runningSleep(andrew.id, FELL_ASLEEP)
  const { played, answer } = await playHousehold(page, sleepingToday(sleep))
  await answer("**/api/sleeps/sleep-1", () => {
    played.today = todayWith()
    return { status: 200, json: { sleep: { ...sleep, deletedAt: NOW, version: 2 }, changed: [["today"]] } }
  })
  await openToday(page)
  const sheet = await openSleep(page)
  await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Delete sleep" }).click()
  await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Delete", exact: true }).click()
  await expect(page.getByText("Sleep deleted")).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: "Undo" })).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.getByText("No sleeps yet")).toBeVisible()
})
```

```ts
import type { Sleep, SleepKind } from "@bt/contracts"
import type { SleepAction } from "../../api/actions.ts"
import { isPending } from "../../api/pending.ts"
import type { Sending } from "../../sheets/sending.ts"

export type SleepState =
  | { readonly kind: "idle" }
  | { readonly kind: "starting" }
  | { readonly kind: "running"; readonly sleep: Sleep }

export const sleepState = (active: Sleep | null): SleepState => {
  if (active === null) return { kind: "idle" }
  return isPending(active.id) ? { kind: "starting" } : { kind: "running", sleep: active }
}

export const sleepWaits = (busy: Sending | null) => busy?.sheet === "sleep"

export const startAction = (actionId: string, kind: SleepKind, startedAt: number): SleepAction => ({
  kind: "startSleep",
  payload: { actionId, kind, startedAt },
})

export const wakeAction = (sleep: Sleep, occurredAt: number): SleepAction => ({
  kind: "wakeSleep",
  id: sleep.id,
  payload: { actionId: crypto.randomUUID(), version: sleep.version, occurredAt },
})

export const runningEdit = (sleep: Sleep, kind: SleepKind, startedAt: number): SleepAction => ({
  kind: "editSleep",
  id: sleep.id,
  payload: { actionId: crypto.randomUUID(), version: sleep.version, kind, startedAt, endedAt: null },
})

export const deleteAction = (sleep: Sleep): SleepAction => ({
  kind: "deleteSleep",
  id: sleep.id,
  payload: { actionId: crypto.randomUUID(), version: sleep.version },
})
```

```ts
import type { SleepKind } from "@bt/contracts"
import { sleepKindAt } from "@bt/domain"
import type { Failed } from "../../sheets/sending.ts"
import { NOW, type Resolved, type When, whenAt } from "../../sheets/when.ts"

export const AFTER_SLEEP = "Pick a time after she fell asleep."

export interface SleepStartSession {
  readonly actionId: string
  readonly when: When
  readonly picked: SleepKind | null
}

export const sleepStartSession = (failed: Failed | null, zone: string): SleepStartSession => {
  const action = failed?.sending.action
  if (action?.kind !== "startSleep") return { actionId: crypto.randomUUID(), when: NOW, picked: null }
  const { actionId, startedAt, kind } = action.payload
  return { actionId, when: whenAt(startedAt, zone), picked: kind }
}

export const kindShown = (picked: SleepKind | null, start: Resolved, zone: string, now: number): SleepKind =>
  picked ?? sleepKindAt(start.ok ? start.instant : now, zone)

export const sentWake = (failed: Failed | null, zone: string): When => {
  const action = failed?.sending.action
  return action?.kind === "wakeSleep" ? whenAt(action.payload.occurredAt, zone) : NOW
}
```

```tsx
import type { SleepKind } from "@bt/contracts"
import { SLEEP_KINDS } from "@bt/domain"
import { useId } from "react"
import { ToggleGroup, ToggleGroupItem } from "../../components/ui/toggle-group.tsx"
import { SLEEP_TITLES } from "../today/sleep-parts.ts"

interface SleepKindToggleProps {
  readonly kind: SleepKind
  readonly disabled?: boolean
  readonly onChange: (kind: SleepKind) => void
}

export const SleepKindToggle = ({ kind, disabled = false, onChange }: SleepKindToggleProps) => {
  const labelId = useId()
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
      <span id={labelId} className="text-sm font-medium">
        Nap or night
      </span>
      <ToggleGroup
        aria-labelledby={labelId}
        value={[kind]}
        onValueChange={([next]) => {
          const picked = SLEEP_KINDS.find((candidate) => candidate === next)
          if (picked !== undefined) onChange(picked)
        }}
        disabled={disabled}
        variant="outline"
        size="lg"
      >
        {SLEEP_KINDS.map((candidate) => (
          <ToggleGroupItem key={candidate} value={candidate}>
            {SLEEP_TITLES[candidate]}
          </ToggleGroupItem>
        ))}
      </ToggleGroup>
    </div>
  )
}
```

```tsx
import type { Sleep } from "@bt/contracts"
import { useState } from "react"
import { Button } from "../../components/ui/button.tsx"
import { clockOf } from "../../sheets/clock.ts"
import { useSheets } from "../../sheets/sheets-context.ts"
import { tapTime } from "../../sheets/tap-time.ts"
import { WhenField } from "../../sheets/when-field.tsx"
import { resolveWhen, sameWhen, type When, whenAt } from "../../sheets/when.ts"
import { useNow } from "../../timer/use-now.ts"
import { runningEdit } from "./sleep-state.ts"

interface SleepStartProps {
  readonly sleep: Sleep
  readonly zone: string
  readonly busy: boolean
}

export const SleepStart = ({ sleep, zone, busy }: SleepStartProps) => {
  const sheets = useSheets()
  const now = useNow()
  const [when, setWhen] = useState<When | null>(null)
  if (when === null) {
    return (
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <span className="text-sm font-medium">Fell asleep</span>
        <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
          <span className="text-base tabular-nums">{clockOf(sleep.startedAt, zone)}</span>
          <Button variant="ghost" size="lg" disabled={busy} onClick={() => setWhen(whenAt(sleep.startedAt, zone))}>
            Change
          </Button>
        </span>
      </div>
    )
  }
  const changed = !sameWhen(when, whenAt(sleep.startedAt, zone))
  const save = () => {
    const resolved = resolveWhen(when, zone, tapTime())
    if (!resolved.ok) return
    sheets.send({ sheet: "sleep", action: runningEdit(sleep, sleep.kind, resolved.instant) })
    setWhen(null)
  }
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
      <WhenField label="Fell asleep" when={when} onChange={setWhen} zone={zone} now={now} />
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
        <Button size="lg" disabled={busy || !changed || !resolveWhen(when, zone, now).ok} onClick={save}>
          Save start
        </Button>
        <Button variant="ghost" size="lg" onClick={() => setWhen(null)}>
          Cancel
        </Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
```

```tsx
import { useState } from "react"
import { Button } from "../../components/ui/button.tsx"
import { FailureNote } from "../../sheets/failure-note.tsx"
import { failedFor, useSheets } from "../../sheets/sheets-context.ts"
import { tapTime } from "../../sheets/tap-time.ts"
import { WhenField } from "../../sheets/when-field.tsx"
import { resolveWhen } from "../../sheets/when.ts"
import { useNow } from "../../timer/use-now.ts"
import { SleepKindToggle } from "./sleep-kind-toggle.tsx"
import { kindShown, sleepStartSession } from "./sleep-session.ts"
import { startAction } from "./sleep-state.ts"

export const StartSleepView = ({ zone }: { readonly zone: string }) => {
  const sheets = useSheets()
  const [session] = useState(() => sleepStartSession(failedFor(sheets, "sleep"), zone))
  const [when, setWhen] = useState(session.when)
  const [picked, setPicked] = useState(session.picked)
  const now = useNow()
  const start = resolveWhen(when, zone, now)
  const begin = () => {
    if (sheets.open !== "sleep") return
    const at = tapTime()
    const resolved = resolveWhen(when, zone, at)
    if (!resolved.ok) return
    const kind = kindShown(picked, resolved, zone, at)
    sheets.send({ sheet: "sleep", action: startAction(session.actionId, kind, resolved.instant) })
  }
  return (
    <>
      <WhenField label="Fell asleep" when={when} onChange={setWhen} zone={zone} now={now} />
      <SleepKindToggle kind={kindShown(picked, start, zone, now)} onChange={setPicked} />
      <Button size="lg" className="h-14 text-lg" disabled={!start.ok} onClick={begin}>
        Start sleep
      </Button>
      <FailureNote sheet="sleep" />
    </>
  )
}
```

```tsx
import type { Sleep } from "@bt/contracts"
import { useState } from "react"
import type { SleepAction } from "../../api/actions.ts"
import { Button } from "../../components/ui/button.tsx"
import { DeleteEntry } from "../../sheets/delete-entry.tsx"
import { FailureNote } from "../../sheets/failure-note.tsx"
import { failedFor, useSheets } from "../../sheets/sheets-context.ts"
import { tapTime } from "../../sheets/tap-time.ts"
import { WhenField } from "../../sheets/when-field.tsx"
import { resolveWhen } from "../../sheets/when.ts"
import { useNow } from "../../timer/use-now.ts"
import { SleepKindToggle } from "./sleep-kind-toggle.tsx"
import { AFTER_SLEEP, sentWake } from "./sleep-session.ts"
import { SleepStart } from "./sleep-start.tsx"
import { deleteAction, runningEdit, sleepWaits, wakeAction } from "./sleep-state.ts"
import { SleepTimer } from "./sleep-timer.tsx"

interface RunningSleepViewProps {
  readonly sleep: Sleep
  readonly zone: string
}

export const RunningSleepView = ({ sleep, zone }: RunningSleepViewProps) => {
  const sheets = useSheets()
  const now = useNow()
  const [woke, setWoke] = useState(() => sentWake(failedFor(sheets, "sleep"), zone))
  const busy = sleepWaits(sheets.busy)
  const act = (action: SleepAction) => sheets.send({ sheet: "sleep", action })
  const wake = () => {
    const resolved = resolveWhen(woke, zone, tapTime(), sleep.startedAt + 1)
    if (resolved.ok) act(wakeAction(sleep, resolved.instant))
  }
  return (
    <>
      <FailureNote sheet="sleep" />
      <SleepTimer sleep={sleep} now={now} />
      <SleepStart sleep={sleep} zone={zone} busy={busy} />
      <SleepKindToggle
        kind={sleep.kind}
        disabled={busy}
        onChange={(kind) => act(runningEdit(sleep, kind, sleep.startedAt))}
      />
      <WhenField
        label="Woke"
        when={woke}
        onChange={setWoke}
        zone={zone}
        now={now}
        earliest={sleep.startedAt + 1}
        tooEarly={AFTER_SLEEP}
      />
      <Button
        size="lg"
        className="h-14 text-lg"
        disabled={busy || !resolveWhen(woke, zone, now, sleep.startedAt + 1).ok}
        onClick={wake}
      >
        Wake up
      </Button>
      <DeleteEntry noun="sleep" emptied={null} disabled={busy} onDelete={() => act(deleteAction(sleep))} />
    </>
  )
}
```

```tsx
import { useMe, useToday } from "../../api/hooks.ts"
import { Sheet } from "../../sheets/sheet.tsx"
import { RunningSleepView } from "./running-sleep-view.tsx"
import { sleepState } from "./sleep-state.ts"
import { StartSleepView } from "./start-sleep-view.tsx"

const SleepContent = () => {
  const { data: me } = useMe()
  const { data: today } = useToday()
  if (me === undefined || today === undefined) return null
  const zone = me.household.timeZone
  const state = sleepState(today.activeSleep)
  switch (state.kind) {
    case "idle":
      return <StartSleepView zone={zone} />
    case "starting":
      return <output className="text-muted-foreground">Starting the sleep…</output>
    case "running":
      return <RunningSleepView key={state.sleep.id} sleep={state.sleep} zone={zone} />
  }
}

export const SleepSheet = () => (
  <Sheet sheet="sleep" title="Sleep">
    <SleepContent />
  </Sheet>
)
```

The four buttons sit in one row, each icon over its label; the sheet reads "Fell asleep", "Nap or night", "Woke", Wake up, Delete sleep.

- [ ] **Step 6: Commit.** `git add -A`, then `git commit -m "Sleep is the quick-action bar's fourth button, its icons stacked over their labels, opening a sheet that starts a sleep or wakes her in two taps, labels it a nap or a night by the clock until a parent picks, moves a running sleep's start, and deletes it"`.

### Task 5: A past sleep, and a finished sleep's own sheet

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/features/sleep/past-sleep.ts`, `sleep-edit.ts`, `sleep-times.tsx`, `past-sleep-form.tsx`, `past-sleep-sheet.tsx`, `edit-sleep-form.tsx`, `edit-sleep-sheet.tsx`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/features/sleep/start-sleep-view.tsx`, `apps/web/src/shell/log-sheets.tsx`, `apps/web/src/features/today/recent-rows.ts`, `apps/web/src/features/history/log-at-sheet.tsx`, `apps/web/src/sheets/sheets-context.ts`, `apps/web/src/features/edit/stale.ts`, `apps/web/src/features/edit/edit-failure.tsx`
- Test: create `apps/web/test/past-sleep.test.ts`, `apps/web/test/sleep-edit.test.ts` and `e2e/sleep-edit.spec.ts`; modify `apps/web/test/today-rows.test.ts` and `e2e/history-log.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 4's `kindShown`, `AFTER_SLEEP`, `SleepKindToggle`, `deleteAction`; Task 2's `logSleep` and `editSleep`; M3.4's `useCachedEntry`, `EditFailure`, `StaleNote`, `staleOf`; M3.7's Log at
- Produces:
  - `features/sleep/past-sleep.ts`: `SleepTimes` (`fellAsleep`, `woke`), `SleepSpan` (`{ ok: true; startedAt; endedAt }` or `{ ok: false; tooLong }`), `spanFrom(start, end)`, `wokeAfter(start)`, `spanOf(times, zone, now)`, `pastSleepSession(failed, now, zone, at)`, `pastSleepEntry(actionId, times, picked, zone, now): NewSleep | null` (P5).
  - `features/sleep/sleep-edit.ts`: `SleepEditSession`, `sleepEditSession(failed, sleep, zone)`, `editedStart`, `editedSpan` (kept times exact, P6), `sleepEdit(session, kind, startedAt, endedAt)`, `sleepDelete(target)`.
  - `SleepTimesFields`, `PastSleepSheet` ("Past sleep") and `EditSleepSheet` ("Edit sleep"), mounted in `LogSheets`; the start view gains "Log a past sleep".
  - `RowSheet` gains `"sleep"` and `"editSleep"`: Today's row opens a running sleep in the Sleep sheet and a finished one in its own sheet.
  - `TimedSheet` gains `"pastSleep"`, and Log at offers Sleep, at the quarter hour tapped.
  - `staleOf` reads a `SleepConflict`'s `STALE_VERSION` (and `deleteSleep` asks "Delete it anyway?"); `EditFailure` takes `"editSleep"`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.** `git checkout 501980f -- apps/web/test/past-sleep.test.ts apps/web/test/sleep-edit.test.ts e2e/sleep-edit.spec.ts`, then `git diff 501980f^ 501980f -- <path> | git apply -3` for `apps/web/test/today-rows.test.ts` and `e2e/history-log.spec.ts`.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest"
import { Offline } from "../src/api/errors.ts"
import { pastSleepEntry, pastSleepSession, spanOf } from "../src/features/sleep/past-sleep.ts"
import { whenAt } from "../src/sheets/when.ts"
import { MINUTE, NOW, ZONE } from "./today-fixtures.ts"

const ACTION_ID = "4b1c2a5e-8f3d-4c6b-9a7e-2d1f0e3c5b6a"
const UUID = expect.stringMatching(/^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/u)
const HOUR = 60 * MINUTE
const times = (start: number, end: number) => ({ fellAsleep: whenAt(start, ZONE), woke: whenAt(end, ZONE) })

describe("a past sleep's first values", () => {
  it("is the hour before now", () => {
    expect(pastSleepSession(null, NOW, ZONE, null)).toEqual({
      actionId: UUID,
      ...times(NOW - HOUR, NOW),
      picked: null,
    })
  })

  it("starts at the slot History's Log at picked, an hour long unless that runs past now", () => {
    expect(pastSleepSession(null, NOW, ZONE, NOW - 5 * HOUR)).toMatchObject(times(NOW - 5 * HOUR, NOW - 4 * HOUR))
    expect(pastSleepSession(null, NOW, ZONE, NOW - 20 * MINUTE)).toMatchObject(times(NOW - 20 * MINUTE, NOW))
  })

  it("comes back as it was sent after a failure, so its retry sends the same sleep", () => {
    const action = {
      kind: "logSleep" as const,
      payload: { actionId: ACTION_ID, kind: "night" as const, startedAt: NOW - 3 * HOUR, endedAt: NOW - HOUR },
    }
    const failed = { sending: { sheet: "pastSleep" as const, action }, error: new Offline() }
    expect(pastSleepSession(failed, NOW, ZONE, null)).toEqual({
      actionId: ACTION_ID,
      ...times(NOW - 3 * HOUR, NOW - HOUR),
      picked: "night",
    })
  })
})

describe("a past sleep's span", () => {
  it("runs from when she fell asleep to when she woke", () => {
    expect(spanOf(times(NOW - 3 * HOUR, NOW - HOUR), ZONE, NOW)).toEqual({
      ok: true,
      startedAt: NOW - 3 * HOUR,
      endedAt: NOW - HOUR,
    })
  })

  it("needs a wake after she fell asleep", () => {
    expect(spanOf(times(NOW - HOUR, NOW - 2 * HOUR), ZONE, NOW)).toEqual({ ok: false, tooLong: false })
  })

  it("lasts a day at most, and says so", () => {
    expect(spanOf(times(NOW - 25 * HOUR, NOW), ZONE, NOW)).toEqual({ ok: false, tooLong: true })
    expect(spanOf(times(NOW - 24 * HOUR, NOW), ZONE, NOW)).toMatchObject({ ok: true })
  })

  it("can't end in the future", () => {
    expect(spanOf(times(NOW - HOUR, NOW + 10 * MINUTE), ZONE, NOW)).toEqual({ ok: false, tooLong: false })
  })
})

describe("the past sleep sent", () => {
  it("is a nap or a night by its start, until a parent picks one", () => {
    const evening = times(Date.UTC(2026, 9, 9, 2, 30), Date.UTC(2026, 9, 9, 12))
    expect(pastSleepEntry(ACTION_ID, evening, null, ZONE, NOW)).toEqual({
      actionId: ACTION_ID,
      kind: "night",
      startedAt: Date.UTC(2026, 9, 9, 2, 30),
      endedAt: Date.UTC(2026, 9, 9, 12),
    })
    expect(pastSleepEntry(ACTION_ID, evening, "nap", ZONE, NOW)?.kind).toBe("nap")
    expect(pastSleepEntry(ACTION_ID, times(NOW, NOW - HOUR), null, ZONE, NOW)).toBeNull()
  })
})
```

```ts
import { SleepConflict } from "@bt/contracts"
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest"
import { Offline } from "../src/api/errors.ts"
import { sentAnyway, staleMessage, staleOf } from "../src/features/edit/stale.ts"
import { editedSpan, sleepDelete, sleepEdit, sleepEditSession } from "../src/features/sleep/sleep-edit.ts"
import type { Display } from "../src/features/today/display.ts"
import type { Sending } from "../src/sheets/sending.ts"
import { whenAt } from "../src/sheets/when.ts"
import { ANDREW, EMILY, MINUTE, NOW, sleptAt, ZONE } from "./today-fixtures.ts"

const ACTION_ID = "4b1c2a5e-8f3d-4c6b-9a7e-2d1f0e3c5b6a"
const UUID = expect.stringMatching(/^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/u)
const FELL_ASLEEP = NOW - 90 * MINUTE + 17_000
const WOKE = NOW - 10 * MINUTE + 41_000
const NAP = sleptAt(FELL_ASLEEP, { id: "sleep-1", endedAt: WOKE, version: 2 })
const DISPLAY: Display = {
  timeZone: ZONE,
  volume: "ml",
  locale: "en-US",
  caregivers: [
    { id: ANDREW, displayName: "Andrew" },
    { id: EMILY, displayName: "Emily" },
  ],
}

describe("a finished sleep's edit", () => {
  const session = sleepEditSession(null, NAP, ZONE)

  it("opens on the sleep as it is, with a fresh actionId and its version", () => {
    expect(session).toEqual({
      actionId: UUID,
      target: { id: "sleep-1", version: 2 },
      startedAt: FELL_ASLEEP,
      endedAt: WOKE,
      times: { fellAsleep: whenAt(FELL_ASLEEP, ZONE), woke: whenAt(WOKE, ZONE) },
      kind: "nap",
    })
  })

  it("keeps the exact times it opened on while they're left alone, so a save of the label alone moves nothing", () => {
    expect(editedSpan(session, session.times, ZONE, NOW)).toEqual({ ok: true, startedAt: FELL_ASLEEP, endedAt: WOKE })
    expect(sleepEdit(session, "night", FELL_ASLEEP, WOKE)).toEqual({
      kind: "editSleep",
      id: "sleep-1",
      payload: { actionId: session.actionId, version: 2, kind: "night", startedAt: FELL_ASLEEP, endedAt: WOKE },
    })
  })

  it("takes a changed time to the minute, and needs the wake after she fell asleep", () => {
    const moved = { ...session.times, woke: whenAt(NOW - 5 * MINUTE, ZONE) }
    expect(editedSpan(session, moved, ZONE, NOW)).toMatchObject({ ok: true, endedAt: NOW - 5 * MINUTE })
    const reversed = { ...session.times, fellAsleep: whenAt(NOW - 5 * MINUTE, ZONE) }
    expect(editedSpan(session, reversed, ZONE, NOW)).toEqual({ ok: false, tooLong: false })
  })

  it("comes back as it was sent after a failure", () => {
    const action = sleepEdit({ ...session, actionId: ACTION_ID }, "night", FELL_ASLEEP, NOW - 5 * MINUTE)
    const resumed = sleepEditSession({ sending: { sheet: "editSleep", action }, error: new Offline() }, NAP, ZONE)
    expect(resumed).toMatchObject({ actionId: ACTION_ID, endedAt: NOW - 5 * MINUTE, kind: "night" })
  })

  it("deletes with the version it opened on and a fresh actionId", () => {
    expect(sleepDelete(session.target)).toEqual({
      kind: "deleteSleep",
      id: "sleep-1",
      payload: { actionId: UUID, version: 2 },
    })
  })
})

describe("a sleep the other phone changed first", () => {
  const theirs = { ...NAP, kind: "night" as const, version: 3, updatedBy: EMILY }
  const sending: Sending = {
    sheet: "editSleep",
    action: sleepEdit(sleepEditSession(null, NAP, ZONE), "nap", FELL_ASLEEP, WOKE),
  }
  const stale = staleOf({ sending, error: new SleepConflict({ code: "STALE_VERSION", current: theirs }) })

  it("says who changed it and how it reads now, and saves mine on their version", () => {
    expect(stale).toEqual({ current: { kind: "sleep", sleep: theirs }, deleting: false })
    const message = stale === null ? "" : staleMessage(DISPLAY, stale, NOW).replace(/\s/gu, " ")
    expect(message).toBe("Emily changed this a moment ago. It now reads: Night at 8:00 AM: 1 h 20 min, Woke 9:20 AM.")
    expect(sentAnyway(sending, { kind: "sleep", sleep: theirs }).action.payload).toMatchObject({ version: 3 })
  })
})
```

```ts
import { expect, type Page, test } from "@playwright/test"
import {
  caregiversOf,
  MINUTE,
  NOW,
  openToday,
  playHousehold,
  type SleepAnswer,
  sleepingToday,
  todayWith,
} from "./played-household.ts"

const ACTION_ID = expect.stringMatching(/^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/u)
const HOUR = 60 * MINUTE
const recent = (page: Page) => page.getByRole("list", { name: "Last 24 hours" })

test.use({ timezoneId: "Europe/London" })

const napOf = (by: string): SleepAnswer => ({
  id: "sleep-1",
  kind: "nap",
  startedAt: NOW - 90 * MINUTE + 17_000,
  endedAt: NOW - 10 * MINUTE + 41_000,
  version: 2,
  createdBy: by,
  createdAt: NOW - 90 * MINUTE,
  updatedBy: by,
  updatedAt: NOW - 10 * MINUTE,
  deletedAt: null,
})

test("logs a past sleep from the sleep sheet: the hour before now, a nap by its start, with Undo", async ({ page }) => {
  const { andrew } = await caregiversOf(page)
  const { played, answer } = await playHousehold(page, todayWith())
  await answer("**/api/sleeps", ({ body }) => {
    const sleep = { ...napOf(andrew.id), startedAt: body.startedAt ?? 0, endedAt: body.endedAt ?? 0, version: 1 }
    played.today = sleepingToday(sleep)
    return { status: 201, json: { sleep, changed: [["today"]] } }
  })
  await openToday(page)
  await page.getByRole("region", { name: "Log", exact: true }).getByRole("button", { name: "Sleep" }).click()
  await page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "Sleep" }).getByRole("button", { name: "Log a past sleep" }).click()
  const sheet = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "Past sleep" })
  await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Save" }).click()
  await expect
    .poll(() => played.sent)
    .toEqual([
      {
        method: "POST",
        path: "/api/sleeps",
        body: { actionId: ACTION_ID, kind: "nap", startedAt: NOW - HOUR, endedAt: NOW },
      },
    ])
  await expect(page.getByText("Sleep saved")).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.getByText("Since she woke")).toBeVisible()
})

test("edits a finished sleep from Today's list, keeping its start to the second, with its version", async ({
  page,
}) => {
  const { andrew } = await caregiversOf(page)
  const nap = napOf(andrew.id)
  const { played, answer } = await playHousehold(page, sleepingToday(nap))
  await answer("**/api/sleeps/sleep-1", ({ body }) => {
    const sleep = { ...nap, kind: body.kind ?? nap.kind, endedAt: body.endedAt ?? nap.endedAt, version: 3 }
    played.today = sleepingToday(sleep)
    return { status: 200, json: { sleep, changed: [["today"]] } }
  })
  await openToday(page)
  await recent(page).getByRole("button", { name: /Nap/u }).click()
  const sheet = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "Edit sleep" })
  await sheet.getByLabel("Woke").fill("2026-10-09T09:25")
  await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Night", exact: true }).click()
  await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Save" }).click()
  await expect(sheet).toBeHidden()
  await expect
    .poll(() => played.sent)
    .toEqual([
      {
        method: "PATCH",
        path: "/api/sleeps/sleep-1",
        body: {
          actionId: ACTION_ID,
          version: 2,
          kind: "night",
          startedAt: nap.startedAt,
          endedAt: Date.UTC(2026, 9, 9, 16, 25),
        },
      },
    ])
  await expect(recent(page)).toContainText("Night")
})

test("says who changed the sleep first, and saves mine on top of theirs when asked", async ({ page }) => {
  const { andrew, emily } = await caregiversOf(page)
  const nap = napOf(andrew.id)
  const theirs = { ...nap, kind: "night" as const, version: 3, updatedBy: emily.id }
  const { played, answer } = await playHousehold(page, sleepingToday(nap))
  await answer("**/api/sleeps/sleep-1", ({ body }) => {
    if (played.sent.length === 1) {
      played.today = sleepingToday(theirs)
      return { status: 409, json: { _tag: "Conflict", code: "STALE_VERSION", current: theirs } }
    }
    const mine = { ...theirs, kind: body.kind ?? theirs.kind, version: 4 }
    played.today = sleepingToday(mine)
    return { status: 200, json: { sleep: mine, changed: [["today"]] } }
  })
  await openToday(page)
  await recent(page).getByRole("button", { name: /Nap/u }).click()
  const sheet = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "Edit sleep" })
  await sheet.getByLabel("Woke").fill("2026-10-09T09:25")
  await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Save" }).click()
  await expect(sheet.getByRole("alert")).toContainText(`${emily.displayName} changed this a moment ago.`)
  await expect(sheet.getByRole("alert")).toContainText(/It now reads: Night at 8:00\sAM/u)
  await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Save mine anyway" }).click()
  await expect(sheet).toBeHidden()
  const [first, second] = played.sent.map(({ body }) => body)
  expect(second).toEqual({ ...first, version: 3 })
})
```
