```ts
import { MAX_SLEEP_MS, type NewSleep, type SleepKind } from "@bt/contracts"
import type { Failed } from "../../sheets/sending.ts"
import { type Resolved, resolveWhen, type When, whenAt } from "../../sheets/when.ts"
import { kindShown } from "./sleep-session.ts"

const HOUR_MS = 60 * 60_000

export interface PastSleepSession {
  readonly actionId: string
  readonly fellAsleep: When
  readonly woke: When
  readonly picked: SleepKind | null
}

export interface SleepTimes {
  readonly fellAsleep: When
  readonly woke: When
}

export type SleepSpan =
  | { readonly ok: true; readonly startedAt: number; readonly endedAt: number }
  | { readonly ok: false; readonly tooLong: boolean }

const NO_SPAN: SleepSpan = { ok: false, tooLong: false }

export const spanFrom = (start: Resolved, end: Resolved): SleepSpan => {
  if (!start.ok || !end.ok || end.instant <= start.instant) return NO_SPAN
  const tooLong = end.instant - start.instant > MAX_SLEEP_MS
  return tooLong ? { ok: false, tooLong } : { ok: true, startedAt: start.instant, endedAt: end.instant }
}

const freshSession = (start: number, now: number, zone: string): PastSleepSession => ({
  actionId: crypto.randomUUID(),
  fellAsleep: whenAt(start, zone),
  woke: whenAt(Math.min(start + HOUR_MS, now), zone),
  picked: null,
})

export const pastSleepSession = (
  failed: Failed | null,
  now: number,
  zone: string,
  at: number | null,
): PastSleepSession => {
  const action = failed?.sending.action
  if (action?.kind !== "logSleep") return freshSession(at ?? now - HOUR_MS, now, zone)
  const { actionId, startedAt, endedAt, kind } = action.payload
  return { actionId, fellAsleep: whenAt(startedAt, zone), woke: whenAt(endedAt, zone), picked: kind }
}

export const wokeAfter = (start: Resolved) => (start.ok ? start.instant + 1 : undefined)

export const spanOf = ({ fellAsleep, woke }: SleepTimes, zone: string, now: number): SleepSpan => {
  const start = resolveWhen(fellAsleep, zone, now)
  return spanFrom(start, resolveWhen(woke, zone, now, wokeAfter(start)))
}

export const pastSleepEntry = (
  actionId: string,
  times: SleepTimes,
  picked: SleepKind | null,
  zone: string,
  now: number,
): NewSleep | null => {
  const span = spanOf(times, zone, now)
  if (!span.ok) return null
  const kind = kindShown(picked, { ok: true, instant: span.startedAt }, zone, now)
  return { actionId, kind, startedAt: span.startedAt, endedAt: span.endedAt }
}
```

```ts
import type { Sleep, SleepKind } from "@bt/contracts"
import type { SleepAction } from "../../api/actions.ts"
import type { Failed } from "../../sheets/sending.ts"
import { type Resolved, resolveWhen, sameWhen, type When, whenAt } from "../../sheets/when.ts"
import { type SleepSpan, type SleepTimes, spanFrom, wokeAfter } from "./past-sleep.ts"

export interface SleepEditSession {
  readonly actionId: string
  readonly target: { readonly id: string; readonly version: number }
  readonly startedAt: number
  readonly endedAt: number
  readonly times: SleepTimes
  readonly kind: SleepKind
}

const sessionOf = (
  actionId: string,
  target: SleepEditSession["target"],
  sleep: Pick<Sleep, "kind" | "startedAt">,
  endedAt: number,
  zone: string,
): SleepEditSession => ({
  actionId,
  target,
  startedAt: sleep.startedAt,
  endedAt,
  times: { fellAsleep: whenAt(sleep.startedAt, zone), woke: whenAt(endedAt, zone) },
  kind: sleep.kind,
})

export const sleepEditSession = (failed: Failed | null, sleep: Sleep, zone: string): SleepEditSession => {
  const action = failed?.sending.action
  if (action?.kind === "editSleep" && action.payload.endedAt !== null) {
    const { actionId, version, endedAt } = action.payload
    return sessionOf(actionId, { id: action.id, version }, action.payload, endedAt, zone)
  }
  const { id, version, startedAt, endedAt } = sleep
  return sessionOf(crypto.randomUUID(), { id, version }, sleep, endedAt ?? startedAt, zone)
}

const kept = (when: When, stored: When, instant: number, resolve: () => Resolved): Resolved =>
  sameWhen(when, stored) ? { ok: true, instant } : resolve()

export const editedStart = (session: SleepEditSession, times: SleepTimes, zone: string, now: number): Resolved =>
  kept(times.fellAsleep, session.times.fellAsleep, session.startedAt, () => resolveWhen(times.fellAsleep, zone, now))

export const editedSpan = (session: SleepEditSession, times: SleepTimes, zone: string, now: number): SleepSpan => {
  const start = editedStart(session, times, zone, now)
  const end = kept(times.woke, session.times.woke, session.endedAt, () =>
    resolveWhen(times.woke, zone, now, wokeAfter(start)),
  )
  return spanFrom(start, end)
}

export const sleepEdit = (
  session: SleepEditSession,
  kind: SleepKind,
  startedAt: number,
  endedAt: number,
): SleepAction => ({
  kind: "editSleep",
  id: session.target.id,
  payload: { actionId: session.actionId, version: session.target.version, kind, startedAt, endedAt },
})

export const sleepDelete = ({ id, version }: SleepEditSession["target"]): SleepAction => ({
  kind: "deleteSleep",
  id,
  payload: { actionId: crypto.randomUUID(), version },
})
```

```tsx
import { WhenField } from "../../sheets/when-field.tsx"
import type { Resolved } from "../../sheets/when.ts"
import { type SleepSpan, type SleepTimes, wokeAfter } from "./past-sleep.ts"
import { AFTER_SLEEP } from "./sleep-session.ts"

interface SleepTimesFieldsProps {
  readonly times: SleepTimes
  readonly start: Resolved
  readonly span: SleepSpan
  readonly zone: string
  readonly now: number
  readonly onChange: (times: SleepTimes) => void
}

export const SleepTimesFields = ({ times, start, span, zone, now, onChange }: SleepTimesFieldsProps) => (
  <>
    <WhenField
      label="Fell asleep"
      when={times.fellAsleep}
      onChange={(fellAsleep) => onChange({ ...times, fellAsleep })}
      zone={zone}
      now={now}
    />
    <WhenField
      label="Woke"
      when={times.woke}
      onChange={(woke) => onChange({ ...times, woke })}
      zone={zone}
      now={now}
      earliest={wokeAfter(start)}
      tooEarly={AFTER_SLEEP}
    />
    {!span.ok && span.tooLong ? <p className="text-sm text-destructive">A sleep can last a day at most.</p> : null}
  </>
)

```tsx
import { useState } from "react"
import { Button } from "../../components/ui/button.tsx"
import { FailureNote } from "../../sheets/failure-note.tsx"
import { failedFor, useSheets } from "../../sheets/sheets-context.ts"
import { tapTime } from "../../sheets/tap-time.ts"
import { resolveWhen } from "../../sheets/when.ts"
import { useNow } from "../../timer/use-now.ts"
import { pastSleepEntry, pastSleepSession, type SleepTimes, spanOf } from "./past-sleep.ts"
import { SleepKindToggle } from "./sleep-kind-toggle.tsx"
import { kindShown } from "./sleep-session.ts"
import { SleepTimesFields } from "./sleep-times.tsx"

export const PastSleepForm = ({ zone }: { readonly zone: string }) => {
  const sheets = useSheets()
  const now = useNow()
  const [session] = useState(() => pastSleepSession(failedFor(sheets, "pastSleep"), now, zone, sheets.at))
  const [times, setTimes] = useState<SleepTimes>({ fellAsleep: session.fellAsleep, woke: session.woke })
  const [picked, setPicked] = useState(session.picked)
  const start = resolveWhen(times.fellAsleep, zone, now)
  const span = spanOf(times, zone, now)
  const save = () => {
    if (sheets.open !== "pastSleep") return
    const payload = pastSleepEntry(session.actionId, times, picked, zone, tapTime())
    if (payload !== null) sheets.send({ sheet: "pastSleep", action: { kind: "logSleep", payload } })
  }
  return (
    <>
      <FailureNote sheet="pastSleep" />
      <SleepTimesFields times={times} start={start} span={span} zone={zone} now={now} onChange={setTimes} />
      <SleepKindToggle kind={kindShown(picked, start, zone, now)} onChange={setPicked} />
      <Button size="lg" className="h-14 text-lg" disabled={!span.ok} onClick={save}>
        Save
      </Button>
    </>
  )
}

```tsx
import type { RecentEntry, Sleep } from "@bt/contracts"
import { useState } from "react"
import { Button } from "../../components/ui/button.tsx"
import { DeleteEntry } from "../../sheets/delete-entry.tsx"
import { failedFor, useSheets } from "../../sheets/sheets-context.ts"
import { tapTime } from "../../sheets/tap-time.ts"
import { useNow } from "../../timer/use-now.ts"
import { EditFailure } from "../edit/edit-failure.tsx"
import type { Display } from "../today/display.ts"
import { editedSpan, editedStart, sleepDelete, sleepEdit, sleepEditSession } from "./sleep-edit.ts"
import { SleepKindToggle } from "./sleep-kind-toggle.tsx"
import { SleepTimesFields } from "./sleep-times.tsx"

interface EditSleepFormProps {
  readonly sleep: Sleep
  readonly display: Display
  readonly onUseTheirs: (current: RecentEntry) => void
}

export const EditSleepForm = ({ sleep, display, onUseTheirs }: EditSleepFormProps) => {
  const sheets = useSheets()
  const zone = display.timeZone
  const [session] = useState(() => sleepEditSession(failedFor(sheets, "editSleep"), sleep, zone))
  const [times, setTimes] = useState(session.times)
  const [kind, setKind] = useState(session.kind)
  const now = useNow()
  const span = editedSpan(session, times, zone, now)
  const start = editedStart(session, times, zone, now)
  const save = () => {
    if (sheets.open !== "editSleep") return
    const at = editedSpan(session, times, zone, tapTime())
    if (at.ok) sheets.send({ sheet: "editSleep", action: sleepEdit(session, kind, at.startedAt, at.endedAt) })
  }
  const remove = () => {
    if (sheets.open === "editSleep") sheets.send({ sheet: "editSleep", action: sleepDelete(session.target) })
  }
  return (
    <>
      <EditFailure sheet="editSleep" display={display} onUseTheirs={onUseTheirs} />
      <SleepTimesFields times={times} start={start} span={span} zone={zone} now={now} onChange={setTimes} />
      <SleepKindToggle kind={kind} onChange={setKind} />
      <Button size="lg" className="h-14 text-lg" disabled={!span.ok} onClick={save}>
        Save
      </Button>
      <DeleteEntry noun="sleep" emptied={null} onDelete={remove} />
    </>
  )
}
```

```tsx
import { useState } from "react"
import { useMe } from "../../api/hooks.ts"
import { Sheet } from "../../sheets/sheet.tsx"
import { useSheets } from "../../sheets/sheets-context.ts"
import { useCachedEntry } from "../edit/use-cached-entry.ts"
import { displayOf } from "../today/display.ts"
import { EditSleepForm } from "./edit-sleep-form.tsx"

const EditSleepContent = () => {
  const sheets = useSheets()
  const { data: me } = useMe()
  const cached = useCachedEntry(sheets.editing)
  const [shown, setShown] = useState({ entry: cached, taken: 0 })
  if (me === undefined) return null
  if (shown.entry?.kind !== "sleep") return <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">This sleep was deleted.</p>
  return (
    <EditSleepForm
      key={shown.taken}
      sleep={shown.entry.sleep}
      display={displayOf(me.household)}
      onUseTheirs={(entry) => setShown(({ taken }) => ({ entry, taken: taken + 1 }))}
    />
  )
}

export const EditSleepSheet = () => (
  <Sheet sheet="editSleep" title="Edit sleep">
    <EditSleepContent />
  </Sheet>
)

```ts
import { expect, type Page, test } from "@playwright/test"
import { busyDay, local, openHistory, playHistory, sleep } from "./played-day.ts"

const grid = (page: Page) => page.getByRole("region", { name: "The day by hour" })

const boxOf = async (page: Page, name: RegExp) => {
  const box = await grid(page).getByRole("button", { name }).boundingBox()
  if (box === null) throw new Error(`No box for ${name.source}`)
  return box
}

const playSleptDay = async (page: Page) => {
  const { played, andrew, emily } = await playHistory(page, [])
  played.days.push({
    ...busyDay(andrew, emily),
    sleeps: [
      sleep("night", local("20:30", "2026-10-08"), local("05:00"), "night", emily),
      sleep("nap", local("07:10"), local("08:20"), "nap", andrew),
    ],
  })
  return played
}

test("draws each sleep in its own column beside the hours, the feeds it overlaps keeping their width", async ({
  page,
}) => {
  await playSleptDay(page)
  await openHistory(page)
  const night = await boxOf(page, /^Night at 8:30\sPM/u)
  const feed = await boxOf(page, /^Nursing at 1:05\sAM/u)
  expect(night.x + night.width).toBeLessThanOrEqual(feed.x)
  expect(feed.width).toBeGreaterThan(120)
  expect(night.y).toBeLessThan(feed.y)
})

test("opens a sleep's details, and its edit sheet from them", async ({ page }) => {
  await playSleptDay(page)
  await openHistory(page)
  await grid(page)
    .getByRole("button", { name: /^Nap at 7:10\sAM/u })
    .click()
  const details = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "Nap" })
  await expect(details).toContainText(/Fri, Oct 9, 7:10\s–\s8:20\sAM/u)
  await expect(details).toContainText("1 h 10 min")
  await expect(details).toContainText("Logged by Andrew (local)")
  await details.getByRole("button", { name: "Edit" }).click()
  const edit = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "Edit sleep" })
  await expect(edit.getByLabel("Fell asleep")).toHaveValue("2026-10-09T07:10")
  await expect(edit.getByLabel("Woke")).toHaveValue("2026-10-09T08:20")
})

test("hides the sleeps behind the Sleep chip, and says so", async ({ page }) => {
  await playSleptDay(page)
  await openHistory(page)
  await expect(grid(page).getByRole("button", { name: /^Nap at/u })).toBeVisible()
  await page.getByRole("group", { name: "Show" }).getByRole("button", { name: "Sleep" }).click()
  await expect(grid(page).getByRole("button", { name: /^Nap at/u })).toHaveCount(0)
  await expect(page.getByText("Sleep hidden")).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/hide=sleep/u)
})

test("fits all four chips on a 360-pixel-wide phone, without scrolling the page sideways", async ({ page }) => {
  await page.setViewportSize({ width: 360, height: 640 })
  await playSleptDay(page)
  await openHistory(page)
  await expect(page.getByRole("group", { name: "Show" }).getByRole("button")).toHaveText([
    "Feeds",
    "Diapers",
    "Sleep",
    "Growth",
  ])
  expect(await page.evaluate("document.documentElement.scrollWidth <= window.innerWidth")).toBe(true)
})
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run them and watch them fail.** `pnpm exec vitest run apps/web/test/history-model.test.ts apps/web/test/history-kinds.test.ts apps/web/test/history-details.test.ts apps/web/test/history-summary.test.ts apps/web/test/history-labels.test.ts` and `pnpm exec playwright test --config .superpowers/e2e.config.ts history-sleep.spec.ts`. Expected: FAIL, as the Day view draws no sleep and has no Sleep chip.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the code.** Invoke `frontend-ui-expert` and `react-code-standards` first. `git checkout fe13bfa -- apps/web/src/features/history/sleep-block.tsx`, then the diffs below with `git apply -3`.

```tsx
import type { PlacedBlock, Side } from "@bt/domain"
import { cn } from "cn"
import { atStep, inLane, stepsTall } from "./grid-classes.ts"

interface SleepBlockProps {
  readonly block: PlacedBlock<Side>
  readonly title: string
  readonly length: string
  readonly name: string
  readonly onOpen: () => void
}

export const SleepBlock = ({ block, title, length, name, onOpen }: SleepBlockProps) => (
  <button
    type="button"
    aria-label={name}
    onClick={onOpen}
    className={cn(
      "group pointer-events-auto absolute px-0.5 text-left outline-none focus-visible:z-20",
      atStep(block.top),
      stepsTall(block.height),
      inLane(block.lane, block.lanes),
    )}
  >
    <span
      className={cn(
        "block size-full overflow-hidden rounded-md border border-muted-foreground/35 bg-muted-foreground/15",
        "group-focus-visible:ring-3 group-focus-visible:ring-ring/50",
        block.startsBefore && "rounded-t-none border-t-0",
        block.endsAfter && "rounded-b-none border-b-0",
      )}
    >
      <span className="block truncate px-0.5 text-[0.6875rem] leading-4 font-medium">{title}</span>
      <span className="block truncate px-0.5 text-[0.6875rem] leading-4 text-muted-foreground tabular-nums">
        {length}
      </span>
    </span>
  </button>
)
```

+    case "feed":

+export const runningSheetOf = (entries: ReadonlyArray<Entry>): EntrySheet | null => {
+  const [only] = entries
+  if (entries.length !== 1 || only === undefined) return null
+  const sheet = editSheetOf(only)
+  return RUNNING_SHEETS.has(sheet) ? sheet : null

 }

+export const markerText = (entry: Entry, volume: VolumeUnit, now: number) => {
+  switch (entry.kind) {
+    case "diaper":
+      return diaperChip(entry.diaper)
+    case "feed":
+      return formatVolume(feedSummary(entry.feed, now).bottleMl, volume)
+    case "sleep":
+      return sleepTitle(entry.sleep)
+  }
+}
 
 const sleepName = (sleep: Sleep, display: Display, now: number) =>
   `${sleepTitle(sleep)} at ${clockTime(sleep.startedAt, display.timeZone, display.locale)}: ${sleepParts(sleep, display.timeZone, now, display.locale).join(", ")}`
@@ -63,3 +71,17 @@ export const entryName = (entry: RecentEntry, display: Display, now: number) =>
 
 export const clusterName = (count: number, first: number, display: Display) =>
   `${count} entries from ${clockTime(first, display.timeZone, display.locale)}`
+
+export const compactLength = (seconds: number) => {
+  if (seconds < MINUTE_S) return "<1m"
+  const minutes = Math.floor(seconds / MINUTE_S)
+  const hours = Math.floor(minutes / HOUR_MIN)
+  const rest = minutes % HOUR_MIN
+  if (hours === 0) return `${minutes}m`
+  return rest === 0 ? `${hours}h` : `${hours}h${String(rest).padStart(2, "0")}`
+}
+
+export const sleepBlockText = (sleep: Sleep, now: number) => ({
+  title: sleepTitle(sleep),
+  length: sleep.endedAt === null ? "now" : compactLength(sleepElapsed(sleep, now)),
+})

- [ ] **Step 6: Commit.** `git add -A`, then `git commit -m "History's Day view draws each sleep in a column of its own between the hours and the feeds, opening its details and its sheet, says the day's sleep under its totals, and hides sleeps behind a Sleep chip"`.

### Task 7: Sleeps on History's Week view

**Files:**
- Modify: `apps/web/src/features/history/week-model.ts`, `week-marks.tsx`, `week-legend.tsx`, `week-names.ts`
- Modify: `e2e/played-week.ts`
- Test: modify `apps/web/test/history-week.test.ts` and `e2e/history-week.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 6's `piecesOf` with `sleeps`, `totalsLines`' sleep line; M3.8's `WEEK_SCALE`, `onWallClock`, `layoutDay`
- Produces:
  - `WeekColumn.sleeps: ReadonlyArray<PlacedBlock<Side>>`, at ten-minute steps on the household's wall clock.
  - `WeekMarks` draws each as a stripe (`data-kind="sleep"`) at the column's left (P10); `WeekLegend` gains "Sleep"; `shownTotals` zeroes sleep behind its chip.
  - `e2e/played-week.ts`: `weekOf` carries its days' sleeps.

+  page,
+}) => {
+  const { played, emily } = await playHistory(page, [])
+  const night = sleep("night", local("19:40", "2026-10-08"), local("06:10"), "night", emily)
+  const thursday = {
+    ...emptyDay("2026-10-08"),
+    sleeps: [night],
+    totals: { ...NO_TOTALS, sleepsStarted: 1, sleepSeconds: 15_600, nightSeconds: 15_600, longestSleepSeconds: 37_800 },
+  }
+  const friday = {
+    ...emptyDay(DAY),
+    sleeps: [night],
+    totals: { ...NO_TOTALS, sleepSeconds: 22_200, nightSeconds: 22_200 },
+  }
+  played.days.push(thursday, friday)
+  await playWeeks(page, { [THIS_WEEK]: weekOf(THIS_WEEK, [thursday, friday]) })
+  await openHistory(page, "?view=week")
+  const thu = weekGrid(page).getByRole("link", { name: "Thu, Oct 8: 4 h 20 min asleep, 4 h 20 min night" })
+  const fri = weekGrid(page).getByRole("link", { name: "Fri, Oct 9: 6 h 10 min asleep, 6 h 10 min night" })
+  await expect(thu).toBeVisible()
+  await expect(fri).toBeVisible()
+  await expect(stripesIn(page, /^Thu, Oct 8/u)).toHaveCount(1)
+  await expect(stripesIn(page, /^Fri, Oct 9/u)).toHaveCount(1)
+  await expect(weekGrid(page).getByText("Sleep", { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
+  await page.getByRole("group", { name: "Show" }).getByRole("button", { name: "Sleep" }).click()
+  await expect(weekGrid(page).getByRole("link", { name: "Fri, Oct 9: nothing shown" })).toBeVisible()
+  await expect(stripesIn(page, /^Fri, Oct 9/u)).toHaveCount(0)
+})

+        <Swatch className="h-3 w-1 rounded-[1px] bg-muted-foreground/45" />
+        Sleep
+      </span>
+    )}
     {hidden.has("growth") ? null : (
       <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1">
         <RulerIcon aria-hidden="true" className="size-3" />
```

### Task 8: Trends' sleep chart

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/domain/src/trends/running.ts`, `apps/web/src/features/trends/metrics.ts`, `apps/web/src/features/trends/day-view.tsx`
- Test: modify `packages/domain/test/trends/running.test.ts`, `apps/web/test/trends-charts.test.ts`, `apps/web/test/trends-rows.test.ts`, `e2e/trends.spec.ts`, `e2e/trends-day.spec.ts`; create `e2e/trends-sleep.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: M3.1's `Entries` and `Totals`' sleep fields, `hasEntries` and `averageDay` already counting sleep; M3.10's `TrendsMetric`, `runningChartModel`, `barRows`
- Produces:
  - `@bt/domain`: `runningTotals(period, entries: Entries, now)` (was `period, feeds, diapers, now`), with `RunningTotals.sleepSeconds`, rounded down once per nap and once per night, so its last point is `totalsIn`'s `sleepSeconds`. Nursing shares the same climb, rounded once per side.
  - `MetricId` gains `"sleep"`; `TRENDS_METRICS` ends with `SLEEP` (P11), so the charts, the numbers and the table gain it.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.** `git checkout 03ca755 -- e2e/trends-sleep.spec.ts`, then `git diff 03ca755^ 03ca755 -- <path> | git apply -3` for the rest.

```ts
import { expect, test } from "@playwright/test"
import { datesFrom, logged, openTrends, periodOf, playTrends } from "./played-period.ts"

test.use({ timezoneId: "America/Vancouver" })

const slept = (naps: number, night: number, longest: number) => ({
  sleepsStarted: 3,
  sleepSeconds: (naps + night) * 60,
  napSeconds: naps * 60,
  nightSeconds: night * 60,
  longestSleepSeconds: longest * 60,
})

const SLEPT_DAYS = {
  "2026-10-05": { ...logged(8, 52, 48, 400, 210, 8), ...slept(180, 600, 360) },
  "2026-10-06": { ...logged(7, 40, 40, 520, 0, 7), ...slept(200, 560, 300) },
}

const WEEK = periodOf(datesFrom("2026-10-05", 7), SLEPT_DAYS)
const OCTOBER = periodOf(datesFrom("2026-10-01", 31), SLEPT_DAYS)

test("charts sleep fifth: hours a day, naps and night, with the longest among its numbers", async ({ page }) => {
  await playTrends(page, { weeks: { "2026-W41": WEEK }, months: { "2026-10": OCTOBER } })
  await openTrends(page)
  const sleep = page.getByRole("region", { name: "Sleep" })
  await expect(sleep).toContainText("12 h 50 min a day, over 2 days")
  await expect(sleep).toContainText(/Naps 3 h 10 min\s·\sNight 9 h 40 min\s·\sLongest 5 h 30 min/u)
  await expect(sleep.getByRole("img")).toHaveAccessibleName("Sleep each day, in hours")
})

test("fits the table's five columns on a phone without scrolling the page sideways", async ({ page }) => {
  await playTrends(page, { weeks: { "2026-W41": WEEK }, months: { "2026-10": OCTOBER } })
  await openTrends(page)
  await page.getByText("Show as a table", { exact: true }).click()
  await expect(
    page
      .getByRole("table")
      .getByRole("row", { name: /Mon, Oct 5/u })
      .getByRole("cell"),
  ).toHaveText(["8", "1 h 40 min", "610 mL", "8", "13 h"])
  expect(await page.evaluate("document.documentElement.scrollWidth <= window.innerWidth")).toBe(true)
})
```

+const sleepSentence = ({ code, current }: SleepConflict, household: Household, running: boolean): string => {
+  switch (code) {
+    case "SLEEP_ALREADY_ACTIVE":
+      return runningOver(
+        `the sleep ${nameOf(household, current.createdBy)} started at ${timeOf(household, current.startedAt)}`,
+      )
+    case "SLEEP_OVERLAP": {
+      const other = `the ${current.kind} at ${spanOf(household, current)}`
+      return running ? runningOver(other) : `This overlaps ${other}.`
+    }
+    case "STALE_VERSION":
+      return staleSentence(household, current)
+    case "ACTION_REPLAY_MISMATCH":
+      return NOT_RESTORED
+  }
+}
+
 const conflictSentence = (error: Error, household: Household, record: DeletedRecord): string | null => {
+  if (error instanceof SleepConflict) return sleepSentence(error, household, stillRunning(record))
   if (error instanceof FeedConflict) return feedSentence(error, household, stillRunning(record))
   if (!(error instanceof DiaperConflict) && !(error instanceof MeasurementConflict)) return null
   return error.code === "STALE_VERSION" ? staleSentence(household, error.current) : NOT_RESTORED
```

-- `calendar/` — `layoutDay(input, scale)` lays a day out for History (M3.7). It draws one row per real hour inside the household's day bounds: fewer when the clocks go forward, and more when they go back a whole hour or more, with each repeated hour marked; on Lord Howe's half-hour changes one row runs 90 minutes, giving 23 rows going forward and 24 going back. History gives it a feed as one block, from its first part to its last, with each side as a band inside it. Blocks sit in lanes (half-open, stable sort by start, end, then id) and are cut at the day's edges. Markers that would overlap gather into one. Everything is in steps of `scale.stepMs`: a block is at least `minBlockSteps` tall and a marker `pointSteps`. `slotIn(row, fraction, now)` gives the quarter hour a tap lands on, or nothing after now. `onWallClock(day)` moves a day onto the household's wall clock for History's Week view (M3.8), so every column has the same 24 rows: on a day the clocks change, both passes of a repeated hour share its rows, a skipped hour stays empty, a side whose clock goes back as it ends is drawn from its start, and a feed's block still covers each of its sides.

Run every gate again: `pnpm typecheck`, `pnpm lint`, `pnpm test`, `pnpm coverage`, the whole E2E suite (`pnpm exec playwright test --config .superpowers/e2e.config.ts`), `pnpm build` then `pnpm bundle:check`.

  - **M3.9 (#70) first:** its list view gets sleep rows, as its hand-off on #44 asks. In `list-rows.ts`, add sleeps to `entriesOf` by their start, give a finished sleep its span in `nursedSpan`'s rule (start to end, a weekday on an end outside the day; a running one shows its start, with "Asleep now" first in its details), and count sleeps in `listNote`.   - She woke at 2:10 and nobody tapped until 2:40: can the parent put the wake right, and what if they pick 1:50, before she fell asleep?
  - Emily started a sleep on her phone a moment before Andrew tapped Start sleep: what does his sheet say, and what's kept?
  - With no signal, a parent taps Start sleep, and iOS closes the app: what happens when it opens again?
  - A night from 7:30 PM to 6 AM, with a feed at 3 AM: what do yesterday's and today's Day view, Week view, Trends and totals each show?
  - On a 360 px phone with larger text: do the four quick actions and the four chips still fit, and does every block keep its words?
